> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# OpenCode Agent Refactoring Plan

## Problem Summary

The OpenCode implementation violates the agent integration checklist:
1. **Creates custom JSONL format** instead of using `opencode export` (native JSON)
2. **ExportData never populated** so rewind doesn't restore OpenCode's database
3. **Two file formats** written by plugin (`.jsonl` and `.export.json`)

## Design Decision

**Store `opencode export` JSON as NativeData.** This is OpenCode's native format. The Go hook handler will call `opencode export` to get the canonical transcript. No backward compatibility needed - integration is brand new.

## Implementation Phases

### Phase 1: Add `runOpenCodeExport` CLI wrapper

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/cli_commands.go`

```go
func runOpenCodeExport(sessionID string) ([]byte, error)
```

Execute `opencode export <session-id>` and capture stdout as JSON bytes.

### Phase 2: Update `ReadTranscript` to use canonical export

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

Change `ReadTranscript(sessionRef)` to:
1. Extract session ID from sessionRef path (filename without extension)
2. Call `runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)` to get native JSON

### Phase 3: Rewrite transcript parsing for export JSON format

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/transcript.go`

Export JSON structure:
```json
{
  "info": { "id": "...", ... },
  "messages": [
    { "info": { "id": "...", "role": "user", ... }, "parts": [...] }
  ]
}
```

Replace all JSONL parsing with export JSON parsing:
- `ParseMessages()` → `ParseExportSession()`
- `ExtractModifiedFiles()` - rewrite for export JSON
- `ExtractAllUserPrompts()` - rewrite for export JSON
- `CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes()` - rewrite for export JSON

Delete JSONL-specific code.

### Phase 4: Update `ReadSession` to populate ExportData

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

```go
func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
    data, err := a.ReadTranscript(input.SessionRef)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    modifiedFiles, _ := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
    return &agent.AgentSession{
        AgentName:     a.Name(),
        SessionID:     input.SessionID,
        SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
        NativeData:    data,  // Export JSON
        ExportData:    data,  // Same - used for opencode import
        ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
    }, nil
}
```

### Phase 5: Simplify `WriteSession`

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

```go
func (a *OpenCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
    if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
        return err
    }
    // NativeData is export JSON - import directly
    if err := a.importSessionIntoOpenCode(session.SessionID, session.NativeData); err != nil {
        fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "warning: could not import session: %v\n", err)
    }
    return nil
}
```

### Phase 6: Update chunking for JSON format

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

Rewrite `ChunkTranscript()` and `ReassembleTranscript()` for JSON (split/merge messages array). Reference Gemini CLI's implementation.

### Phase 7: Simplify plugin - remove all JSONL code

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/entire_plugin.ts`

Delete:
- `writeTranscriptFromMemory()`
- `writeTranscriptWithFallback()`
- `formatMessageFromStore()`
- `formatMessageFromAPI()`
- All `.jsonl` references

The plugin only needs to pass session_id to hooks. Go calls `opencode export` directly.

Update `session.idle`:
```typescript
case "session.idle": {
    const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
    if (!sessionID) break
    await callHook("turn-end", { session_id: sessionID })
    break
}
```

### Phase 8: Remove special-case in lifecycle.go

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go`

Delete lines 209-218 (`.export.json` copying logic). The transcript IS the export data now.

### Phase 9: Update types.go

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/types.go`

- Add `ExportSession` struct for top-level export JSON
- Keep/adapt `Message` and `Part` types for the nested structure
- Delete JSONL-specific types if any

## Files to Modify

1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/cli_commands.go` - Add `runOpenCodeExport`
2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go` - ReadTranscript, ReadSession, WriteSession, chunking
3. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/transcript.go` - Rewrite all parsing for export JSON
4. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/types.go` - Add ExportSession, clean up
5. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/entire_plugin.ts` - Delete JSONL code, simplify
6. `cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` - Remove export.json special-case

## Verification

1. **Unit tests**: Rewrite tests in `transcript_test.go` for export JSON
2. **Integration tests**: Update `integration_test/opencode_hooks_test.go`
3. **Manual testing**:
   - Start OpenCode session, make changes, verify checkpoint captures export JSON
   - Rewind to checkpoint, verify `opencode -s <id>` resumes correctly
4. **Run**: `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`

     1→// Package opencode implements the Agent interface for OpenCode.
     2→package opencode
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"errors"
     6→	"fmt"
     7→	"os"
     8→	"path/filepath"
     9→	"regexp"
    10→	"strings"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    14→)
    15→
    16→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    17→func init() {
    18→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameOpenCode, NewOpenCodeAgent)
    19→}
    20→
    21→//nolint:revive // OpenCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    22→type OpenCodeAgent struct{}
    23→
    24→// NewOpenCodeAgent creates a new OpenCode agent instance.
    25→func NewOpenCodeAgent() agent.Agent {
    26→	return &OpenCodeAgent{}
    27→}
    28→
    29→// --- Identity ---
    30→
    31→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName   { return agent.AgentNameOpenCode }
    32→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Type() agent.AgentType   { return agent.AgentTypeOpenCode }
    33→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) Description() string     { return "OpenCode - AI-powered terminal coding agent" }
    34→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) IsPreview() bool         { return true }
    35→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".opencode"} }
    36→
    37→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    38→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		repoRoot = "."
    41→	}
    42→	// Check for .opencode directory or opencode.json config
    43→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".opencode")); err == nil {
    44→		return true, nil
    45→	}
    46→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(repoRoot, "opencode.json")); err == nil {
    47→		return true, nil
    48→	}
    49→	return false, nil
    50→}
    51→
    52→// --- Transcript Storage ---
    53→
    54→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    55→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    56→	if err != nil {
    57→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read opencode transcript: %w", err)
    58→	}
    59→	return data, nil
    60→}
    61→
    62→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
    63→	// OpenCode uses JSONL (one message per line) — use the shared JSONL chunker.
    64→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk opencode transcript: %w", err)
    67→	}
    68→	return chunks, nil
    69→}
    70→
    71→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
    72→	// JSONL reassembly is simple concatenation.
    73→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
    74→}
    75→
    76→// --- Legacy methods ---
    77→
    78→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
    79→	return input.SessionID
    80→}
    81→
    82→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Entire stores OpenCode session transcripts.
    83→// Transcripts are ephemeral handoff files between the TS plugin and the Go hook handler.
    84→// Once checkpointed, the data lives on git refs and the file is disposable.
    85→// Stored in os.TempDir()/entire-opencode/<sanitized-path>/ to avoid squatting on
    86→// OpenCode's own directories (~/.opencode/ is project-level, not home-level).
    87→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
    88→	// Check for test environment override
    89→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
    90→		return override, nil
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	projectDir := SanitizePathForOpenCode(repoPath)
    94→	return filepath.Join(os.TempDir(), "entire-opencode", projectDir), nil
    95→}
    96→
    97→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
    98→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
    99→}
   100→
   101→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   102→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   103→		return nil, errors.New("no session ref provided")
   104→	}
   105→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   111→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   114→		modifiedFiles = nil
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   118→		AgentName:     a.Name(),
   119→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   120→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   121→		NativeData:    data,
   122→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   123→	}, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   127→	if session == nil {
   128→		return errors.New("nil session")
   129→	}
   130→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   131→		return errors.New("no session ref to write to")
   132→	}
   133→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   134→		return errors.New("no session data to write")
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// 1. Write JSONL file (for Entire's internal checkpoint use)
   138→	dir := filepath.Dir(session.SessionRef)
   139→	//nolint:gosec // G301: Session directory needs standard permissions
   140→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   141→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   142→	}
   143→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   144→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session data: %w", err)
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	// 2. If we have export data, import the session into OpenCode.
   148→	//    This enables `opencode -s <id>` for both resume and rewind.
   149→	if len(session.ExportData) == 0 {
   150→		return nil // No export data — skip import (graceful degradation)
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	if err := a.importSessionIntoOpenCode(session.SessionID, session.ExportData); err != nil {
   154→		// Non-fatal: import is best-effort. The JSONL file is written,
   155→		// and the user can always run `opencode import <file>` manually.
   156→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "warning: could not import session into OpenCode: %v\n", err)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	return nil
   160→}
   161→
   162→// importSessionIntoOpenCode writes the export JSON to a temp file and runs
   163→// `opencode import` to restore the session into OpenCode's database.
   164→// For rewind (session already exists), the session is deleted first so the
   165→// reimport replaces it with the checkpoint-state messages.
   166→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) importSessionIntoOpenCode(sessionID string, exportData []byte) error {
   167→	// Delete the session first so reimport replaces it cleanly.
   168→	// opencode import uses ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, so existing messages
   169→	// would be skipped without this step (breaking rewind).
   170→	// runOpenCodeSessionDelete treats "not found" as success.
   171→	if err := runOpenCodeSessionDelete(sessionID); err != nil {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to delete existing session: %w", err)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Write export JSON to a temp file for opencode import
   176→	tmpFile, err := os.CreateTemp("", "entire-opencode-export-*.json")
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp file: %w", err)
   179→	}
   180→	defer os.Remove(tmpFile.Name())
   181→
   182→	if _, err := tmpFile.Write(exportData); err != nil {
   183→		_ = tmpFile.Close()
   184→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write export data: %w", err)
   185→	}
   186→	if err := tmpFile.Close(); err != nil {
   187→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to close temp file: %w", err)
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	return runOpenCodeImport(tmpFile.Name())
   191→}
   192→
   193→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   194→	if strings.TrimSpace(sessionID) == "" {
   195→		return "opencode"
   196→	}
   197→	return "opencode -s " + sessionID
   198→}
   199→
   200→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character.
   201→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   202→
   203→// SanitizePathForOpenCode converts a path to a safe directory name.
   204→// Replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash (same approach as Claude/Gemini).
   205→func SanitizePathForOpenCode(path string) string {
   206→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   207→}
   208→

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"slices"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// Compile-time interface assertions
    16→var (
    17→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    18→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)
    19→)
    20→
    21→// ParseMessages parses JSONL content (one Message per line) into a slice of Messages.
    22→func ParseMessages(data []byte) ([]Message, error) {
    23→	if len(data) == 0 {
    24→		return nil, nil
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	var messages []Message
    28→	reader := bufio.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(data))
    29→
    30→	for {
    31→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    32→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    33→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read opencode transcript: %w", err)
    34→		}
    35→
    36→		if len(bytes.TrimSpace(lineBytes)) > 0 {
    37→			var msg Message
    38→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &msg); jsonErr == nil {
    39→				messages = append(messages, msg)
    40→			}
    41→		}
    42→
    43→		if err == io.EOF {
    44→			break
    45→		}
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	return messages, nil
    49→}
    50→
    51→// parseMessagesFromFile reads and parses a JSONL transcript file.
    52→func parseMessagesFromFile(path string) ([]Message, error) {
    53→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // Callers check os.IsNotExist on this error
    56→	}
    57→	return ParseMessages(data)
    58→}
    59→
    60→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of JSONL lines in the transcript.
    61→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    62→	messages, err := parseMessagesFromFile(path)
    63→	if err != nil {
    64→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    65→			return 0, nil
    66→		}
    67→		return 0, err
    68→	}
    69→	return len(messages), nil
    70→}
    71→
    72→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified by tool calls from the given message offset.
    73→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
    74→	messages, err := parseMessagesFromFile(path)
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    77→			return nil, 0, nil
    78→		}
    79→		return nil, 0, err
    80→	}
    81→
    82→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
    83→	var files []string
    84→
    85→	for i := startOffset; i < len(messages); i++ {
    86→		msg := messages[i]
    87→		if msg.Role != roleAssistant {
    88→			continue
    89→		}
    90→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
    91→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
    92→				continue
    93→			}
    94→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
    95→				continue
    96→			}
    97→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
    98→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
    99→				seen[filePath] = true
   100→				files = append(files, filePath)
   101→			}
   102→		}
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	return files, len(messages), nil
   106→}
   107→
   108→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts modified file paths from raw JSONL transcript bytes.
   109→// This is the bytes-based equivalent of ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, used by ReadSession.
   110→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   111→	messages, err := ParseMessages(data)
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		return nil, err
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   117→	var files []string
   118→
   119→	for _, msg := range messages {
   120→		if msg.Role != roleAssistant {
   121→			continue
   122→		}
   123→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   124→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   125→				continue
   126→			}
   127→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   128→				continue
   129→			}
   130→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   131→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   132→				seen[filePath] = true
   133→				files = append(files, filePath)
   134→			}
   135→		}
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	return files, nil
   139→}
   140→
   141→// extractFilePathFromInput extracts the file path from a tool's input map.
   142→func extractFilePathFromInput(input map[string]interface{}) string {
   143→	for _, key := range []string{"file_path", "path", "file", "filename"} {
   144→		if v, ok := input[key]; ok {
   145→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   146→				return s
   147→			}
   148→		}
   149→	}
   150→	return ""
   151→}
   152→
   153→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompt strings from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   154→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
   155→	messages, err := parseMessagesFromFile(sessionRef)
   156→	if err != nil {
   157→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   158→			return nil, nil
   159→		}
   160→		return nil, err
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	var prompts []string
   164→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(messages); i++ {
   165→		msg := messages[i]
   166→		if msg.Role == roleUser && msg.Content != "" {
   167→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	return prompts, nil
   172→}
   173→
   174→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message content as a summary.
   175→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
   176→	messages, err := parseMessagesFromFile(sessionRef)
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   179→			return "", nil
   180→		}
   181→		return "", err
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	for i := len(messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   185→		msg := messages[i]
   186→		if msg.Role == roleAssistant && msg.Content != "" {
   187→			return msg.Content, nil
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	return "", nil
   192→}
   193→
   194→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from raw JSONL transcript bytes.
   195→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path.
   196→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   197→	messages, err := ParseMessages(data)
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return nil, err
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	var prompts []string
   203→	for _, msg := range messages {
   204→		if msg.Role == roleUser && msg.Content != "" {
   205→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
   206→		}
   207→	}
   208→	return prompts, nil
   209→}
   210→
   211→// CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes computes token usage from raw JSONL transcript bytes
   212→// starting at the given message offset.
   213→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path (which has bytes, not a file path).
   214→func CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   215→	messages, err := ParseMessages(data)
   216→	if err != nil || messages == nil {
   217→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   221→	for i := startMessageIndex; i < len(messages); i++ {
   222→		msg := messages[i]
   223→		if msg.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Tokens == nil {
   224→			continue
   225→		}
   226→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Tokens.Input
   227→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Tokens.Output
   228→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Tokens.Cache.Read
   229→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Tokens.Cache.Write
   230→		usage.APICallCount++
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return usage
   234→}
   235→
   236→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from assistant messages starting at the given offset.
   237→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   238→	messages, err := parseMessagesFromFile(sessionRef)
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   241→			return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for nonexistent file is expected
   242→		}
   243→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript for token usage: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   247→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(messages); i++ {
   248→		msg := messages[i]
   249→		if msg.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Tokens == nil {
   250→			continue
   251→		}
   252→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Tokens.Input
   253→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Tokens.Output
   254→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Tokens.Cache.Read
   255→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Tokens.Cache.Write
   256→		usage.APICallCount++
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return usage, nil
   260→}
   261→

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→// sessionInfoRaw matches the JSON payload piped from the OpenCode plugin for session events.
     4→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
     5→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
     6→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
     7→}
     8→
     9→// turnStartRaw matches the JSON payload for turn-start (user prompt submission).
    10→type turnStartRaw struct {
    11→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    12→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    13→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
    14→}
    15→
    16→// --- Transcript types (JSONL format — one Message per line) ---
    17→
    18→// Message role constants.
    19→const (
    20→	roleAssistant = "assistant"
    21→	roleUser      = "user"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// Message represents a single message (one line) in the JSONL transcript.
    25→type Message struct {
    26→	ID      string  `json:"id"`
    27→	Role    string  `json:"role"` // "user" or "assistant"
    28→	Content string  `json:"content"`
    29→	Time    Time    `json:"time"`
    30→	Tokens  *Tokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
    31→	Cost    float64 `json:"cost,omitempty"`
    32→	Parts   []Part  `json:"parts,omitempty"`
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Time holds message timestamps.
    36→type Time struct {
    37→	Created   int64 `json:"created"`
    38→	Completed int64 `json:"completed,omitempty"`
    39→}
    40→
    41→// Tokens holds token usage from assistant messages.
    42→type Tokens struct {
    43→	Input     int   `json:"input"`
    44→	Output    int   `json:"output"`
    45→	Reasoning int   `json:"reasoning"`
    46→	Cache     Cache `json:"cache"`
    47→}
    48→
    49→// Cache holds cache-related token counts.
    50→type Cache struct {
    51→	Read  int `json:"read"`
    52→	Write int `json:"write"`
    53→}
    54→
    55→// Part represents a message part (text, tool, etc.).
    56→type Part struct {
    57→	Type   string     `json:"type"` // "text", "tool", etc.
    58→	Text   string     `json:"text,omitempty"`
    59→	Tool   string     `json:"tool,omitempty"`
    60→	CallID string     `json:"callID,omitempty"`
    61→	State  *ToolState `json:"state,omitempty"`
    62→}
    63→
    64→// ToolState represents tool execution state.
    65→type ToolState struct {
    66→	Status string                 `json:"status"` // "pending", "running", "completed", "error"
    67→	Input  map[string]interface{} `json:"input,omitempty"`
    68→	Output string                 `json:"output,omitempty"`
    69→}
    70→
    71→// FileModificationTools are tools in OpenCode that modify files on disk.
    72→// These match the actual tool names from OpenCode's source:
    73→//   - edit:  internal/llm/tools/edit.go  (EditToolName)
    74→//   - write: internal/llm/tools/write.go (WriteToolName)
    75→//   - patch: internal/llm/tools/patch.go (PatchToolName)
    76→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    77→	"edit",
    78→	"write",
    79→	"patch",
    80→}
    81→

     1→// Entire CLI plugin for OpenCode
     2→// Auto-generated by `entire enable --agent opencode`
     3→// Do not edit manually — changes will be overwritten on next install.
     4→// Requires Bun runtime (used by OpenCode's plugin system for loading ESM plugins).
     5→import type { Plugin } from "@opencode-ai/plugin"
     6→import { tmpdir } from "node:os"
     7→
     8→export const EntirePlugin: Plugin = async ({ client, directory, $ }) => {
     9→  const ENTIRE_CMD = "__ENTIRE_CMD__"
    10→  // Store transcripts in a temp directory — these are ephemeral handoff files
    11→  // between the plugin and the Go hook handler. Once checkpointed, the data
    12→  // lives on git refs and the file is disposable.
    13→  const sanitized = directory.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "-")
    14→  const transcriptDir = `${tmpdir()}/entire-opencode/${sanitized}`
    15→  const seenUserMessages = new Set<string>()
    16→
    17→  // In-memory stores — used to write transcripts without relying on the SDK API,
    18→  // which may be unavailable during shutdown.
    19→  // messageStore: keyed by message ID, stores message metadata (role, time, tokens, etc.)
    20→  const messageStore = new Map<string, any>()
    21→  // partStore: keyed by message ID, stores accumulated parts from message.part.updated events
    22→  const partStore = new Map<string, any[]>()
    23→  let currentSessionID: string | null = null
    24→  // Full session info from session.created — needed for OpenCode export format on resume/rewind
    25→  let currentSessionInfo: any = null
    26→
    27→  // Ensure transcript directory exists
    28→  await $`mkdir -p ${transcriptDir}`.quiet().nothrow()
    29→
    30→  /**
    31→   * Pipe JSON payload to an entire hooks command.
    32→   * Errors are logged but never thrown — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode.
    33→   */
    34→  async function callHook(hookName: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>) {
    35→    try {
    36→      const json = JSON.stringify(payload)
    37→      await $`echo ${json} | ${ENTIRE_CMD} hooks opencode ${hookName}`.quiet().nothrow()
    38→    } catch {
    39→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
    40→    }
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  /** Extract text content from a list of parts. */
    44→  function textFromParts(parts: any[]): string {
    45→    return parts
    46→      .filter((p: any) => p.type === "text")
    47→      .map((p: any) => p.text ?? "")
    48→      .join("\n")
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  /** Format a message object from its accumulated parts. */
    52→  function formatMessageFromStore(msg: any) {
    53→    const parts = partStore.get(msg.id) ?? []
    54→    return {
    55→      id: msg.id,
    56→      role: msg.role,
    57→      content: textFromParts(parts),
    58→      time: msg.time,
    59→      ...(msg.role === "assistant" ? {
    60→        tokens: msg.tokens,
    61→        cost: msg.cost,
    62→        parts: parts.map((p: any) => ({
    63→          type: p.type,
    64→          ...(p.type === "text" ? { text: p.text } : {}),
    65→          ...(p.type === "tool" ? { tool: p.tool, callID: p.callID, state: p.state } : {}),
    66→        })),
    67→      } : {}),
    68→    }
    69→  }
    70→
    71→  /** Format a message from an API response (which includes parts inline). */
    72→  function formatMessageFromAPI(info: any, parts: any[]) {
    73→    return {
    74→      id: info.id,
    75→      role: info.role,
    76→      content: textFromParts(parts),
    77→      time: info.time,
    78→      ...(info.role === "assistant" ? {
    79→        tokens: info.tokens,
    80→        cost: info.cost,
    81→        parts: parts.map((p: any) => ({
    82→          type: p.type,
    83→          ...(p.type === "text" ? { text: p.text } : {}),
    84→          ...(p.type === "tool" ? { tool: p.tool, callID: p.callID, state: p.state } : {}),
    85→        })),
    86→      } : {}),
    87→    }
    88→  }
    89→
    90→  /**
    91→   * Write transcript as JSONL (one message per line) from in-memory stores.
    92→   * This does NOT call the SDK API, so it works even during shutdown.
    93→   */
    94→  async function writeTranscriptFromMemory(sessionID: string): Promise<string> {
    95→    const transcriptPath = `${transcriptDir}/${sessionID}.jsonl`
    96→    try {
    97→      const messages = Array.from(messageStore.values())
    98→        .sort((a, b) => (a.time?.created ?? 0) - (b.time?.created ?? 0))
    99→
   100→      const lines = messages.map(msg => JSON.stringify(formatMessageFromStore(msg)))
   101→      await Bun.write(transcriptPath, lines.join("\n") + "\n")
   102→    } catch {
   103→      // Silently ignore write failures
   104→    }
   105→    return transcriptPath
   106→  }
   107→
   108→  /**
   109→   * Try to fetch messages via the SDK API (returns messages with parts inline)
   110→   * and write transcript as JSONL. Falls back to in-memory stores if the API is unavailable.
   111→   */
   112→  async function writeTranscriptWithFallback(sessionID: string): Promise<string> {
   113→    const transcriptPath = `${transcriptDir}/${sessionID}.jsonl`
   114→    try {
   115→      const response = await client.session.message.list({ path: { id: sessionID } })
   116→      // API returns Array<{ info: Message, parts: Array<Part> }>
   117→      const items = response.data ?? []
   118→
   119→      const lines = items.map((item: any) =>
   120→        JSON.stringify(formatMessageFromAPI(item.info, item.parts ?? []))
   121→      )
   122→      await Bun.write(transcriptPath, lines.join("\n") + "\n")
   123→      return transcriptPath
   124→    } catch {
   125→      // API unavailable (likely shutting down) — fall back to in-memory stores
   126→      return writeTranscriptFromMemory(sessionID)
   127→    }
   128→  }
   129→
   130→  /**
   131→   * Write session in OpenCode's native export format (JSON).
   132→   * This file is used by `opencode import` during resume/rewind to restore
   133→   * the session into OpenCode's SQLite database with the original session ID.
   134→   */
   135→  async function writeExportJSON(sessionID: string): Promise<string> {
   136→    const exportPath = `${transcriptDir}/${sessionID}.export.json`
   137→    try {
   138→      const messages = Array.from(messageStore.values())
   139→        .sort((a, b) => (a.time?.created ?? 0) - (b.time?.created ?? 0))
   140→
   141→      const exportData = {
   142→        info: currentSessionInfo ?? { id: sessionID },
   143→        messages: messages.map(msg => ({
   144→          info: msg,
   145→          parts: (partStore.get(msg.id) ?? []),
   146→        })),
   147→      }
   148→      await Bun.write(exportPath, JSON.stringify(exportData))
   149→    } catch {
   150→      // Silently ignore — plugin failures must not crash OpenCode
   151→    }
   152→    return exportPath
   153→  }
   154→
   155→  return {
   156→    event: async ({ event }) => {
   157→      switch (event.type) {
   158→        case "session.created": {
   159→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
   160→          if (!session?.id) break
   161→          currentSessionID = session.id
   162→          currentSessionInfo = session
   163→          await callHook("session-start", {
   164→            session_id: session.id,
   165→            transcript_path: `${transcriptDir}/${session.id}.jsonl`,
   166→          })
   167→          break
   168→        }
   169→
   170→        case "message.updated": {
   171→          const msg = (event as any).properties?.info
   172→          if (!msg) break
   173→          // Store message metadata (role, time, tokens, etc.)
   174→          // Content is NOT on the message — it arrives via message.part.updated events.
   175→          messageStore.set(msg.id, msg)
   176→          break
   177→        }
   178→
   179→        case "message.part.updated": {
   180→          const part = (event as any).properties?.part
   181→          if (!part?.messageID) break
   182→
   183→          // Accumulate parts per message
   184→          const existing = partStore.get(part.messageID) ?? []
   185→          // Replace existing part with same id, or append new one
   186→          const idx = existing.findIndex((p: any) => p.id === part.id)
   187→          if (idx >= 0) {
   188→            existing[idx] = part
   189→          } else {
   190→            existing.push(part)
   191→          }
   192→          partStore.set(part.messageID, existing)
   193→
   194→          // Fire turn-start on the first text part of a new user message
   195→          const msg = messageStore.get(part.messageID)
   196→          if (msg?.role === "user" && part.type === "text" && !seenUserMessages.has(msg.id)) {
   197→            seenUserMessages.add(msg.id)
   198→            const sessionID = msg.sessionID ?? currentSessionID
   199→            if (sessionID) {
   200→              await callHook("turn-start", {
   201→                session_id: sessionID,
   202→                transcript_path: `${transcriptDir}/${sessionID}.jsonl`,
   203→                prompt: part.text ?? "",
   204→              })
   205→            }
   206→          }
   207→          break
   208→        }
   209→
   210→        case "session.idle": {
   211→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
   212→          if (!sessionID) break
   213→          const transcriptPath = await writeTranscriptWithFallback(sessionID)
   214→          await writeExportJSON(sessionID)
   215→          await callHook("turn-end", {
   216→            session_id: sessionID,
   217→            transcript_path: transcriptPath,
   218→          })
   219→          break
   220→        }
   221→
   222→        case "session.compacted": {
   223→          const sessionID = (event as any).properties?.sessionID
   224→          if (!sessionID) break
   225→          await callHook("compaction", {
   226→            session_id: sessionID,
   227→            transcript_path: `${transcriptDir}/${sessionID}.jsonl`,
   228→          })
   229→          break
   230→        }
   231→
   232→        case "session.deleted": {
   233→          const session = (event as any).properties?.info
   234→          if (!session?.id) break
   235→          // Write final transcript + export JSON before signaling session end
   236→          if (messageStore.size > 0) {
   237→            await writeTranscriptFromMemory(session.id)
   238→            await writeExportJSON(session.id)
   239→          }
   240→          seenUserMessages.clear()
   241→          messageStore.clear()
   242→          partStore.clear()
   243→          currentSessionID = null
   244→          currentSessionInfo = null
   245→          await callHook("session-end", {
   246→            session_id: session.id,
   247→            transcript_path: `${transcriptDir}/${session.id}.jsonl`,
   248→          })
   249→          break
   250→        }
   251→      }
   252→    },
   253→  }
   254→}
   255→

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Copy export JSON if it exists alongside the transcript (written by OpenCode plugin).
   210→	// This is used by `opencode import` during resume/rewind to restore the session
   211→	// into OpenCode's SQLite database with the original session ID.
   212→	exportSrc := strings.TrimSuffix(transcriptRef, filepath.Ext(transcriptRef)) + ".export.json"
   213→	if exportData, readErr := os.ReadFile(exportSrc); readErr == nil { //nolint:gosec // Path derived from agent hook
   214→		exportDest := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ExportDataFileName)
   215→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(exportDest, exportData, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   216→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to write export data: %v\n", writeErr)
   217→		}
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   221→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	// Determine transcript offset
   227→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   228→
   229→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   230→	var allPrompts []string
   231→	var summary string
   232→	var modifiedFiles []string
   233→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   234→
   235→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   236→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   237→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   238→
   239→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   240→		// Extract prompts
   241→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   242→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   243→		} else {
   244→			allPrompts = prompts
   245→		}
   246→
   247→		// Extract summary
   248→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   249→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   250→		} else {
   251→			summary = s
   252→		}
   253→
   254→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   255→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   256→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→			}
   261→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   262→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   263→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   264→			}
   265→		} else {
   266→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   267→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   268→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   269→			} else {
   270→				modifiedFiles = files
   271→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   272→			}
   273→		}
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Write prompts file
   277→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   278→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   279→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   280→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   281→	}
   282→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   283→
   284→	// Write summary file
   285→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   286→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   287→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   288→	}
   289→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   290→
   291→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   292→	lastPrompt := ""
   293→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   294→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   295→	}
   296→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   297→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   298→
   299→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   300→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   301→	if err != nil {
   302→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   306→	if preState != nil {
   307→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   308→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Detect file changes via git status
   312→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   313→	if err != nil {
   314→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   318→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   320→	if changes != nil {
   321→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   322→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   326→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   327→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   328→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   329→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   330→
   331→	// Check if there are any changes
   332→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   333→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   334→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   335→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   336→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   337→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   338→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   339→		}
   340→		return nil
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Log file changes
   344→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   345→
   346→	// Create context file
   347→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   348→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   349→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   350→	}
   351→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   352→
   353→	// Get git author
   354→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   355→	if err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Get strategy and agent type
   360→	strat := GetStrategy()
   361→	agentType := ag.Type()
   362→
   363→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   364→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   365→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   366→	if preState != nil {
   367→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   368→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   369→	}
   370→
   371→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   372→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   373→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   374→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   375→		if tokenErr != nil {
   376→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   377→		} else {
   378→			tokenUsage = usage
   379→		}
   380→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   381→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   382→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   383→		if tokenErr != nil {
   384→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   385→		} else {
   386→			tokenUsage = usage
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   391→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   392→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   393→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   394→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   395→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   396→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   397→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   398→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   399→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   400→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   401→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   402→		AgentType:                agentType,
   403→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   404→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   405→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   406→	}
   407→
   408→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   409→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   413→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   414→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	// Transition session phase and cleanup
   418→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   419→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   420→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	return nil
   424→}
   425→
   426→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   427→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   428→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   429→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   430→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   431→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   432→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   433→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   434→	)
   435→
   436→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   437→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   438→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   439→	if loadErr != nil {
   440→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   441→	}
   442→	if sessionState != nil {
   443→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   444→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   445→		}
   446→
   447→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   448→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   449→
   450→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   451→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   456→	return nil
   457→}
   458→
   459→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   460→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   461→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   462→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   463→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   464→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   465→	)
   466→
   467→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   468→		return nil // No session to update
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   472→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   473→	}
   474→	return nil
   475→}
   476→
   477→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   478→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   479→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   480→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   481→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   482→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   483→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   484→	)
   485→
   486→	// Log context
   487→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   488→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   489→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   490→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   491→
   492→	// Capture pre-task state
   493→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   494→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	return nil
   498→}
   499→
   500→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   501→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   502→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   503→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   504→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   505→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   506→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   507→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   508→	)
   509→
   510→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   511→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   512→
   513→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   514→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   515→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   516→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   517→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   518→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   519→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   520→		}
   521→	}
   522→
   523→	// Log context
   524→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   525→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   526→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   527→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   528→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   529→	}
   530→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   531→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   532→	}
   533→
   534→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   535→	var modifiedFiles []string
   536→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   537→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   538→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   539→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   540→		}
   541→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   542→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   543→		} else {
   544→			modifiedFiles = files
   545→		}
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   549→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   550→	if err != nil {
   551→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   552→	}
   553→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   554→	if preState != nil {
   555→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   556→	}
   557→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   558→	if err != nil {
   559→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   563→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   564→	if err != nil {
   565→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   566→	}
   567→
   568→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   569→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   570→	if changes != nil {
   571→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   572→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   573→	}
   574→
   575→	// If no changes, skip
   576→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   577→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   578→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   579→		return nil
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   583→	var checkpointUUID string
   584→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   585→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   586→	if mainLines != nil {
   587→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Get git author
   591→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   592→	if err != nil {
   593→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Build task checkpoint context
   597→	strat := GetStrategy()
   598→	agentType := ag.Type()
   599→
   600→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   601→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   602→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   603→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   604→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   605→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   606→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   607→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   608→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   609→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   610→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   611→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   612→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   613→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   614→		AgentType:              agentType,
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   618→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   622→	return nil
   623→}
   624→
   625→// --- Helper functions ---
   626→
   627→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   628→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   629→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   630→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   631→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   632→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   636→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   637→	if loadErr != nil {
   638→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   639→		return 0
   640→	}
   641→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   642→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   643→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   644→	}
   645→
   646→	return 0
   647→}
   648→
   649→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   650→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   651→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   652→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   653→	if loadErr != nil {
   654→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   655→		return
   656→	}
   657→	if sessionState == nil {
   658→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   659→			SessionID: sessionID,
   660→		}
   661→	}
   662→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   663→	sessionState.StepCount++
   664→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   665→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   666→	} else {
   667→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   668→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   669→	}
   670→}
   671→
   672→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   673→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   674→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   675→	var sb strings.Builder
   676→
   677→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   678→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   679→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   680→
   681→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   682→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   683→		for i, p := range prompts {
   684→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   685→		}
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	if summary != "" {
   689→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   690→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   691→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   695→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   696→	}
   697→	return nil
   698→}
   699→
   700→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   701→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   702→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   703→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   704→	if err != nil {
   705→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   706→	}
   707→	return lines, total, nil
   708→}
   709→
   710→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   711→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   712→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   713→	if loadErr != nil {
   714→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   715→		return
   716→	}
   717→	if turnState == nil {
   718→		return
   719→	}
   720→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   721→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   722→	}
   723→
   724→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   725→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   726→	strat := GetStrategy()
   727→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   728→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   729→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   730→		}
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   734→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   735→	}
   736→}
   737→
   738→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   739→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   740→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   741→	if err != nil {
   742→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   743→	}
   744→	if state == nil {
   745→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   746→	}
   747→
   748→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   749→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	now := time.Now()
   753→	state.EndedAt = &now
   754→
   755→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   756→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   757→	}
   758→	return nil
   759→}
   760→
   761→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   762→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   763→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   764→	for _, file := range modified {
   765→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   766→	}
   767→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   768→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   769→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   770→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   771→		}
   772→	}
   773→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   774→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   775→		for _, file := range deleted {
   776→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   777→		}
   778→	}
   779→}
   780→

     1→package paths
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"os/exec"
     8→	"path/filepath"
     9→	"regexp"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"sync"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/id"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// Directory constants
    17→const (
    18→	EntireDir         = ".entire"
    19→	EntireTmpDir      = ".entire/tmp"
    20→	EntireMetadataDir = ".entire/metadata"
    21→)
    22→
    23→// Metadata file names
    24→const (
    25→	ContextFileName          = "context.md"
    26→	PromptFileName           = "prompt.txt"
    27→	SummaryFileName          = "summary.txt"
    28→	TranscriptFileName       = "full.jsonl"
    29→	TranscriptFileNameLegacy = "full.log"
    30→	MetadataFileName         = "metadata.json"
    31→	CheckpointFileName       = "checkpoint.json"
    32→	ContentHashFileName      = "content_hash.txt"
    33→	ExportDataFileName       = "export.json"
    34→	SettingsFileName         = "settings.json"
    35→)
    36→
    37→// MetadataBranchName is the orphan branch used by auto-commit and manual-commit strategies to store metadata
    38→const MetadataBranchName = "entire/checkpoints/v1"
    39→
    40→// CheckpointPath returns the sharded storage path for a checkpoint ID.
    41→// Uses first 2 characters as shard (256 buckets), remaining as folder name.
    42→// Example: "a3b2c4d5e6f7" -> "a3/b2c4d5e6f7"
    43→//
    44→// Deprecated: Use checkpointID.Path() directly instead.
    45→func CheckpointPath(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
    46→	return checkpointID.Path()
    47→}
    48→
    49→// repoRootCache caches the repository root to avoid repeated git commands.
    50→// The cache is keyed by the current working directory to handle directory changes.
    51→var (
    52→	repoRootMu       sync.RWMutex
    53→	repoRootCache    string
    54→	repoRootCacheDir string
    55→)
    56→
    57→// RepoRoot returns the git repository root directory.
    58→// Uses 'git rev-parse --show-toplevel' which works from any subdirectory.
    59→// The result is cached per working directory.
    60→// Returns an error if not inside a git repository.
    61→func RepoRoot() (string, error) {
    62→	// Get current working directory to check cache validity
    63→	cwd, err := os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // already present in codebase
    64→	if err != nil {
    65→		cwd = ""
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	// Check cache with read lock first
    69→	repoRootMu.RLock()
    70→	if repoRootCache != "" && repoRootCacheDir == cwd {
    71→		cached := repoRootCache
    72→		repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    73→		return cached, nil
    74→	}
    75→	repoRootMu.RUnlock()
    76→
    77→	// Cache miss - get repo root and update cache with write lock
    78→	ctx := context.Background()
    79→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
    80→	output, err := cmd.Output()
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git repository root: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	root := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
    86→
    87→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    88→	repoRootCache = root
    89→	repoRootCacheDir = cwd
    90→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
    91→
    92→	return root, nil
    93→}
    94→
    95→// ClearRepoRootCache clears the cached repository root.
    96→// This is primarily useful for testing when changing directories.
    97→func ClearRepoRootCache() {
    98→	repoRootMu.Lock()
    99→	repoRootCache = ""
   100→	repoRootCacheDir = ""
   101→	repoRootMu.Unlock()
   102→}
   103→
   104→// AbsPath returns the absolute path for a relative path within the repository.
   105→// If the path is already absolute, it is returned as-is.
   106→// Uses RepoRoot() to resolve paths relative to the repository root.
   107→func AbsPath(relPath string) (string, error) {
   108→	if filepath.IsAbs(relPath) {
   109→		return relPath, nil
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	root, err := RepoRoot()
   113→	if err != nil {
   114→		return "", err
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	return filepath.Join(root, relPath), nil
   118→}
   119→
   120→// IsInfrastructurePath returns true if the path is part of CLI infrastructure
   121→// (i.e., inside the .entire directory)
   122→func IsInfrastructurePath(path string) bool {
   123→	return strings.HasPrefix(path, EntireDir+"/") || path == EntireDir
   124→}
   125→
   126→// ToRelativePath converts an absolute path to relative.
   127→// Returns empty string if the path is outside the working directory.
   128→func ToRelativePath(absPath, cwd string) string {
   129→	if !filepath.IsAbs(absPath) {
   130→		return absPath
   131→	}
   132→	relPath, err := filepath.Rel(cwd, absPath)
   133→	if err != nil || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
   134→		return ""
   135→	}
   136→	return relPath
   137→}
   138→
   139→// nonAlphanumericRegex matches any non-alphanumeric character
   140→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   141→
   142→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   143→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   144→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   145→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   146→}
   147→
   148→// GetClaudeProjectDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts
   149→// for the given repository path.
   150→//
   151→// In test environments, set ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR to override the default location.
   152→func GetClaudeProjectDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   153→	override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR")
   154→	if override != "" {
   155→		return override, nil
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   164→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   165→}
   166→
   167→// SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID returns the path to a session's metadata directory
   168→// for the given Entire session ID. The sessionID must be the full, already date-prefixed
   169→// Entire session identifier as stored on disk, not an agent-specific or raw Claude ID.
   170→func SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID string) string {
   171→	return EntireMetadataDir + "/" + sessionID
   172→}
   173→
   174→// ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath attempts to extract a session ID from a transcript path.
   175→// Claude transcripts are stored at ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   176→// If the path doesn't match expected format, returns empty string.
   177→func ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(transcriptPath string) string {
   178→	// Try to extract from typical path: ~/.claude/projects/<project>/sessions/<id>.jsonl
   179→	parts := strings.Split(filepath.ToSlash(transcriptPath), "/")
   180→	for i, part := range parts {
   181→		if part == "sessions" && i+1 < len(parts) {
   182→			// Return filename without extension
   183→			filename := parts[i+1]
   184→			if strings.HasSuffix(filename, ".jsonl") {
   185→				return strings.TrimSuffix(filename, ".jsonl")
   186→			}
   187→			return filename
   188→		}
   189→	}
   190→	return ""
   191→}
   192→
