     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os/exec"
     7→	"strings"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// openCodeCommandTimeout is the maximum time to wait for opencode CLI commands.
    12→const openCodeCommandTimeout = 30 * time.Second
    13→
    14→// runOpenCodeSessionDelete runs `opencode session delete <sessionID>` to remove
    15→// a session from OpenCode's database. Treats "Session not found" as success
    16→// (nothing to delete).
    17→func runOpenCodeSessionDelete(sessionID string) error {
    18→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)
    19→	defer cancel()
    20→
    21→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "session", "delete", sessionID)
    22→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
    23→	if err != nil {
    24→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
    25→			return fmt.Errorf("opencode session delete timed out after %s", openCodeCommandTimeout)
    26→		}
    27→		// Treat "Session not found" as success — nothing to delete.
    28→		if strings.Contains(string(output), "Session not found") {
    29→			return nil
    30→		}
    31→		return fmt.Errorf("opencode session delete failed: %w (output: %s)", err, string(output))
    32→	}
    33→	return nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// runOpenCodeImport runs `opencode import <file>` to import a session into
    37→// OpenCode's database. The import preserves the original session ID
    38→// from the export file.
    39→func runOpenCodeImport(exportFilePath string) error {
    40→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)
    41→	defer cancel()
    42→
    43→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "opencode", "import", exportFilePath)
    44→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
    45→		if ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {
    46→			return fmt.Errorf("opencode import timed out after %s", openCodeCommandTimeout)
    47→		}
    48→		return fmt.Errorf("opencode import failed: %w (output: %s)", err, string(output))
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	return nil
    52→}
    53→

     1→package opencode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"os"
     5→	"path/filepath"
     6→	"testing"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// testTranscriptJSONL is a JSONL transcript with 4 messages (one per line).
    12→const testTranscriptJSONL = `{"id":"msg-1","role":"user","content":"Fix the bug in main.go","time":{"created":1708300000}}
    13→{"id":"msg-2","role":"assistant","content":"I'll fix the bug.","time":{"created":1708300001,"completed":1708300005},"tokens":{"input":150,"output":80,"reasoning":10,"cache":{"read":5,"write":15}},"cost":0.003,"parts":[{"type":"text","text":"I'll fix the bug."},{"type":"tool","tool":"edit","callID":"call-1","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"file_path":"main.go"},"output":"Applied edit"}}]}
    14→{"id":"msg-3","role":"user","content":"Also fix util.go","time":{"created":1708300010}}
    15→{"id":"msg-4","role":"assistant","content":"Done fixing util.go.","time":{"created":1708300011,"completed":1708300015},"tokens":{"input":200,"output":100,"reasoning":5,"cache":{"read":10,"write":20}},"cost":0.005,"parts":[{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call-2","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"file_path":"util.go"},"output":"File written"}},{"type":"text","text":"Done fixing util.go."}]}
    16→`
    17→
    18→func writeTestTranscript(t *testing.T, content string) string {
    19→	t.Helper()
    20→	dir := t.TempDir()
    21→	path := filepath.Join(dir, "test-session.jsonl")
    22→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("failed to write test transcript: %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→	return path
    26→}
    27→
    28→func TestParseMessages(t *testing.T) {
    29→	t.Parallel()
    30→
    31→	messages, err := ParseMessages([]byte(testTranscriptJSONL))
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    34→	}
    35→	if len(messages) != 4 {
    36→		t.Fatalf("expected 4 messages, got %d", len(messages))
    37→	}
    38→	if messages[0].ID != "msg-1" {
    39→		t.Errorf("expected first message ID 'msg-1', got %q", messages[0].ID)
    40→	}
    41→	if messages[0].Role != "user" {
    42→		t.Errorf("expected first message role 'user', got %q", messages[0].Role)
    43→	}
    44→}
    45→
    46→func TestParseMessages_Empty(t *testing.T) {
    47→	t.Parallel()
    48→
    49→	messages, err := ParseMessages(nil)
    50→	if err != nil {
    51→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    52→	}
    53→	if messages != nil {
    54→		t.Errorf("expected nil for nil data, got %d messages", len(messages))
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	messages, err = ParseMessages([]byte(""))
    58→	if err != nil {
    59→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    60→	}
    61→	if messages != nil {
    62→		t.Errorf("expected nil for empty data, got %d messages", len(messages))
    63→	}
    64→}
    65→
    66→func TestParseMessages_InvalidLines(t *testing.T) {
    67→	t.Parallel()
    68→
    69→	// Invalid lines are silently skipped
    70→	data := "not json\n{\"id\":\"msg-1\",\"role\":\"user\",\"content\":\"hello\"}\n"
    71→	messages, err := ParseMessages([]byte(data))
    72→	if err != nil {
    73→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    74→	}
    75→	if len(messages) != 1 {
    76→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 valid message, got %d", len(messages))
    77→	}
    78→	if messages[0].Content != "hello" {
    79→		t.Errorf("expected content 'hello', got %q", messages[0].Content)
    80→	}
    81→}
    82→
    83→func TestGetTranscriptPosition(t *testing.T) {
    84→	t.Parallel()
    85→	ag := &OpenCodeAgent{}
    86→	path := writeTestTranscript(t, testTranscriptJSONL)
    87→
    88→	pos, err := ag.GetTranscriptPosition(path)
    89→	if err != nil {
    90→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
    91→	}
    92→	if pos != 4 {
    93→		t.Errorf("expected position 4 (4 messages), got %d", pos)
    94→	}
    95→}
    96→
    97→func TestGetTranscriptPosition_NonexistentFile(t *testing.T) {
    98→	t.Parallel()
    99→	ag := &OpenCodeAgent{}
   100→
   101→	pos, err := ag.GetTranscriptPosition("/nonexistent/path.jsonl")
   102→	if err != nil {
   103→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   104→	}
   105→	if pos != 0 {

   282→func TestChunkTranscript_SplitsLargeContent(t *testing.T) {
   283→	t.Parallel()
   284→	ag := &OpenCodeAgent{}
   285→	content := []byte(testTranscriptJSONL)
   286→
   287→	// Use a maxSize that fits individual lines but forces splitting (assistant lines are ~370-400 bytes)
   288→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, 500)
   289→	if err != nil {
   290→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   291→	}
   292→	if len(chunks) < 2 {
   293→		t.Fatalf("expected multiple chunks for small maxSize, got %d", len(chunks))
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Each chunk should contain valid JSONL
   297→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   298→		messages, parseErr := ParseMessages(chunk)
   299→		if parseErr != nil {
   300→			t.Fatalf("chunk %d: failed to parse: %v", i, parseErr)
   301→		}
   302→		if len(messages) == 0 {
   303→			t.Errorf("chunk %d: expected at least 1 message", i)
   304→		}
   305→	}
   306→}

   308→func TestChunkTranscript_RoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
   309→	t.Parallel()
   310→	ag := &OpenCodeAgent{}
   311→	content := []byte(testTranscriptJSONL)
   312→
   313→	// Split into chunks (maxSize must fit individual JSONL lines)
   314→	chunks, err := ag.ChunkTranscript(content, 500)
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		t.Fatalf("chunk error: %v", err)
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	// Reassemble
   320→	reassembled, err := ag.ReassembleTranscript(chunks)
   321→	if err != nil {
   322→		t.Fatalf("reassemble error: %v", err)
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	// Parse both and compare messages
   326→	original, parseErr := ParseMessages(content)
   327→	if parseErr != nil {
   328→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse original: %v", parseErr)
   329→	}
   330→	result, parseErr := ParseMessages(reassembled)
   331→	if parseErr != nil {
   332→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse reassembled: %v", parseErr)
   333→	}
   334→
   335→	if len(result) != len(original) {
   336→		t.Fatalf("message count mismatch: %d vs %d", len(result), len(original))
   337→	}
   338→	for i, msg := range result {
   339→		if msg.ID != original[i].ID {
   340→			t.Errorf("message %d: ID mismatch %q vs %q", i, msg.ID, original[i].ID)
   341→		}
   342→	}
   343→}

     1→// Package geminicli implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
     2→package geminicli
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"

    20→
    21→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    22→func init() {
    23→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameGemini, NewGeminiCLIAgent)
    24→}
    25→
    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
    27→//
    28→//nolint:revive // GeminiCLIAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    29→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}
    30→
    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &GeminiCLIAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    37→	return agent.AgentNameGemini
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.
    41→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {
    42→	return agent.AgentTypeGemini
    43→}
    44→
    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    46→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Description() string {
    47→	return "Gemini CLI - Google's AI coding assistant"
    48→}
    49→
    50→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) IsPreview() bool { return true }
    51→
    52→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    53→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    54→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    55→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    56→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    59→		repoRoot = "."
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Check for .gemini directory
    63→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    64→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    65→		return true, nil
    66→	}
    67→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    68→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    69→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    70→		return true, nil
    71→	}
    72→	return false, nil
    73→}
    74→
    75→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
    76→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
    77→	return input.SessionID
    78→}
    79→
    80→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
    81→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
    82→
    83→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
    84→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
    85→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
    86→// back to constructing a filename matching Gemini's convention if no match is found.
    87→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
    88→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
    89→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
    90→	shortID := agentSessionID
    91→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
    92→		shortID = shortID[:8]
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
    96→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
    97→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
    98→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
    99→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Fallback: construct filename matching Gemini's convention: session-<timestamp>-<id[:8]>.json
   103→	timestamp := time.Now().UTC().Format("2006-01-02T15-04")
   104→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-"+timestamp+"-"+shortID+".json")
   105→}
   106→
   107→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   108→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   109→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   110→	// Check for test environment override
   111→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   112→		return override, nil
   113→	}
   114→
   115→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	// Gemini uses a SHA256 hash of the project path for the directory name
   121→	projectDir := GetProjectHash(repoPath)
   122→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   123→}
   124→
   125→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   126→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   127→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   128→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   129→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	// Read the raw JSON file
   133→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   134→	if err != nil {
   135→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   139→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   140→	if err != nil {
   141→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   142→		modifiedFiles = nil
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   146→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   147→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   148→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   149→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   150→		NativeData:    data,
   151→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   152→	}, nil
   153→}
   154→
   155→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   156→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   157→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   158→	if session == nil {
   159→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   163→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   164→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   168→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   172→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Write the raw JSON data
   176→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   177→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	return nil
   181→}
   182→
   183→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   184→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   185→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   186→}
   187→
   188→// GetProjectHash generates a unique hash for a project based on its root path.
   189→// This matches Gemini CLI's getProjectHash() which uses SHA256 of the project root.
   190→func GetProjectHash(projectRoot string) string {
   191→	hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(projectRoot))
   192→	return hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
   193→}
   194→
   195→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   196→
   197→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   198→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   199→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   200→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   201→	if path == "" {
   202→		return 0, nil
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   208→			return 0, nil
   209→		}
   210→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	if len(data) == 0 {
   214→		return 0, nil
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   223→}
   224→
   225→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   226→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   227→// Returns:
   228→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   229→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   230→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   231→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   232→	if path == "" {
   233→		return nil, 0, nil
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   237→	if readErr != nil {
   238→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   239→			return nil, 0, nil
   240→		}
   241→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	if len(data) == 0 {
   245→		return nil, 0, nil
   246→	}
   247→
   248→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   249→	if parseErr != nil {
   250→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   254→
   255→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   256→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   257→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   258→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   259→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   260→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   261→			continue
   262→		}
   263→
   264→		// Process tool calls in this message
   265→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   266→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   267→			isModifyTool := false
   268→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   269→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   270→					isModifyTool = true
   271→					break
   272→				}
   273→			}
   274→
   275→			if !isModifyTool {
   276→				continue
   277→			}
   278→
   279→			// Extract file path from args map
   280→			var file string
   281→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   282→				file = fp
   283→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   284→				file = p
   285→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   286→				file = fn
   287→			}
   288→
   289→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   290→				fileSet[file] = true
   291→				files = append(files, file)
   292→			}
   293→		}
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   297→}
   298→
   299→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   300→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   301→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   302→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   303→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   304→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   305→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   306→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   307→		if chunkErr != nil {
   308→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   309→		}
   310→		return chunks, nil
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   314→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	var chunks [][]byte
   318→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   319→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   320→
   321→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   322→		// Marshal message to get its size
   323→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   324→		if err != nil {
   325→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   326→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   327→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   328→			)
   329→			continue
   330→		}
   331→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   332→
   333→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   334→			// Save current chunk
   335→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   336→			if err != nil {
   337→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   338→			}
   339→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   340→
   341→			// Start new chunk
   342→			currentMessages = nil
   343→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   344→		}
   345→
   346→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   347→		currentSize += msgSize
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	// Add the last chunk
   351→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   352→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   353→		if err != nil {
   354→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   355→		}
   356→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   360→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   361→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	return chunks, nil
   365→}
   366→
   367→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   368→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   369→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   370→
   371→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   372→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   373→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   374→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   375→		}
   376→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return result, nil
   384→}
   385→

     3→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure
     4→type GeminiSettings struct {
     5→	HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig `json:"hooksConfig,omitempty"`
     6→	Hooks       GeminiHooks       `json:"hooks,omitempty"`
     7→}
     8→
     9→// GeminiHooksConfig contains tool-related settings
    10→type GeminiHooksConfig struct {
    11→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled,omitempty"`
    12→}
    13→
    14→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations
    15→type GeminiHooks struct {
    16→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    17→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    18→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    19→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    20→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    24→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    25→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    26→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    27→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    28→}
    29→
    30→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    31→type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    32→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    33→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.
    37→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini CLI requires a "name" field for each hook entry.
    38→type GeminiHookEntry struct {
    39→	Name    string `json:"name"`
    40→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    41→	Command string `json:"command"`
    42→}
    43→
    44→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd hooks
    45→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    46→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    47→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    48→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    49→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    50→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    51→	Source         string `json:"source,omitempty"` // For SessionStart: startup, resume, clear
    52→	Reason         string `json:"reason,omitempty"` // For SessionEnd: exit, logout
    53→}
    54→
    55→// agentHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeAgent/AfterAgent hooks.
    56→// BeforeAgent includes the user's prompt, similar to Claude's UserPromptSubmit.
    57→type agentHookInputRaw struct {
    58→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    59→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    60→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    61→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    62→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    63→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt,omitempty"` // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
    64→}
    65→
    66→// Tool names used in Gemini CLI that modify files
    67→// Note: Gemini CLI uses different names in different contexts:
    68→// - Internal/transcript names: write_file, replace
    69→// - Display names: WriteFile, Edit
    70→const (
    71→	ToolWriteFile = "write_file"
    72→	ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    73→	ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    74→	ToolReplace   = "replace"
    75→)
    76→
    77→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in Gemini CLI
    78→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    79→	ToolWriteFile,
    80→	ToolEditFile,
    81→	ToolSaveFile,
    82→	ToolReplace,
    83→}
    84→
    85→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.
    86→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message has a tokens object.
    87→type geminiMessageTokens struct {
    88→	Input    int `json:"input"`
    89→	Output   int `json:"output"`
    90→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
    91→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
    92→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`

    94→}
    95→
    96→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.
    97→// Used for extracting token counts from Gemini transcripts.
    98→type geminiMessageWithTokens struct {
    99→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   100→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   101→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   102→}

     1→package geminicli
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"strings"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    10→)
    11→
    12→// Transcript parsing types - Gemini CLI uses JSON format for session storage
    13→// Based on transcript_path format: ~/.gemini/tmp/<hash>/chats/session-<date>-<id>.json
    14→
    15→// Message type constants for Gemini transcripts
    16→const (
    17→	MessageTypeUser   = "user"
    18→	MessageTypeGemini = "gemini"
    19→)
    20→
    21→// GeminiTranscript represents the top-level structure of a Gemini session file
    22→type GeminiTranscript struct {
    23→	Messages []GeminiMessage `json:"messages"`
    24→}
    25→
    26→// GeminiMessage represents a single message in the transcript
    27→type GeminiMessage struct {
    28→	ID        string           `json:"id,omitempty"` // UUID for the message
    29→	Type      string           `json:"type"`         // MessageTypeUser or MessageTypeGemini
    30→	Content   string           `json:"content,omitempty"`
    31→	ToolCalls []GeminiToolCall `json:"toolCalls,omitempty"`
    32→}
    33→
    34→// UnmarshalJSON handles both string and array content formats in Gemini transcripts.
    35→// User messages use: "content": [{"text": "..."}] (array of objects)
    36→// Gemini messages use: "content": "response text" (string)
    37→func (m *GeminiMessage) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
    38→	// Use an alias to avoid infinite recursion
    39→	type Alias GeminiMessage
    40→	aux := &struct {
    41→		*Alias
    42→
    43→		Content json.RawMessage `json:"content,omitempty"`
    44→	}{
    45→		Alias: (*Alias)(m),
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, aux); err != nil {
    49→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal message: %w", err)
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	if len(aux.Content) == 0 || string(aux.Content) == "null" {
    53→		m.Content = ""
    54→		return nil
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Try string first (most common for gemini messages)
    58→	var strContent string
    59→	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &strContent); err == nil {
    60→		m.Content = strContent
    61→		return nil
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	// Try array of objects with "text" fields (user messages)
    65→	var parts []struct {
    66→		Text string `json:"text"`
    67→	}
    68→	if err := json.Unmarshal(aux.Content, &parts); err == nil {
    69→		var texts []string
    70→		for _, p := range parts {
    71→			if p.Text != "" {
    72→				texts = append(texts, p.Text)
    73→			}
    74→		}
    75→		m.Content = strings.Join(texts, "\n")
    76→		return nil
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Unknown format - leave content empty
    80→	return nil
    81→}
    82→
    83→// GeminiToolCall represents a tool call in a gemini message
    84→type GeminiToolCall struct {
    85→	ID     string                 `json:"id"`
    86→	Name   string                 `json:"name"`
    87→	Args   map[string]interface{} `json:"args"`
    88→	Status string                 `json:"status,omitempty"`
    89→}
    90→
    91→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSON content into a transcript structure
    92→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) (*GeminiTranscript, error) {
    93→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
    94→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
    95→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    96→	}
    97→	return &transcript, nil
    98→}
    99→
   100→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript data
   101→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   102→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   103→	if err != nil {
   104→		return nil, err
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript extracts files from a parsed transcript
   111→func ExtractModifiedFilesFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) []string {
   112→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   113→	var files []string
   114→
   115→	for _, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   116→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   117→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   118→			continue
   119→		}
   120→
   121→		// Process tool calls in this message
   122→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   123→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   124→			isModifyTool := false
   125→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   126→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   127→					isModifyTool = true
   128→					break
   129→				}
   130→			}
   131→
   132→			if !isModifyTool {
   133→				continue
   134→			}
   135→
   136→			// Extract file path from args map
   137→			var file string
   138→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   139→				file = fp
   140→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   141→				file = p
   142→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   143→				file = fn
   144→			}
   145→
   146→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   147→				fileSet[file] = true
   148→				files = append(files, file)
   149→			}
   150→		}
   151→	}
   152→
   153→	return files
   154→}
   155→
   156→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript data
   157→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(data []byte) (string, error) {
   158→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		return "", err
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	return ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   164→}
   165→
   166→// ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript extracts the last user prompt from a parsed transcript
   167→func ExtractLastUserPromptFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   168→	for i := len(transcript.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   169→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   170→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeUser {
   171→			continue
   172→		}
   173→
   174→		// Content is now a string field
   175→		if msg.Content != "" {
   176→			return msg.Content
   177→		}
   178→	}
   179→	return ""
   180→}
   181→
   182→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user messages from transcript data
   183→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   184→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   185→	if err != nil {
   186→		return nil, err
   187→	}
   188→
   189→	return ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   190→}
   191→
   192→// ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript extracts all user prompts from a parsed transcript
   193→func ExtractAllUserPromptsFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) []string {
   194→	var prompts []string
   195→	for _, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   196→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
   197→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
   198→		}
   199→	}
   200→	return prompts
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ExtractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last gemini response from transcript data
   204→func ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data []byte) (string, error) {
   205→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   206→	if err != nil {
   207→		return "", err
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   211→}
   212→
   213→// ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript extracts the last gemini response from a parsed transcript
   214→func ExtractLastAssistantMessageFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   215→	for i := len(transcript.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   216→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   217→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeGemini && msg.Content != "" {
   218→			return msg.Content
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→	return ""
   222→}
   223→
   224→// GetLastMessageID returns the ID of the last message in the transcript.
   225→// Returns empty string if the transcript is empty or the last message has no ID.
   226→func GetLastMessageID(data []byte) (string, error) {
   227→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   228→	if err != nil {
   229→		return "", err
   230→	}
   231→	return GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript(transcript), nil
   232→}
   233→
   234→// GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript returns the ID of the last message in a parsed transcript.
   235→// Returns empty string if the transcript is empty or the last message has no ID.
   236→func GetLastMessageIDFromTranscript(transcript *GeminiTranscript) string {
   237→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   238→		return ""
   239→	}
   240→	return transcript.Messages[len(transcript.Messages)-1].ID
   241→}
   242→
   243→// GetLastMessageIDFromFile reads a transcript file and returns the last message's ID.
   244→// Returns empty string if the file doesn't exist, is empty, or has no messages with IDs.
   245→func GetLastMessageIDFromFile(path string) (string, error) {
   246→	if path == "" {
   247→		return "", nil
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   253→			return "", nil
   254→		}
   255→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	if len(data) == 0 {
   259→		return "", nil
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	return GetLastMessageID(data)
   263→}
   264→
   265→// SliceFromMessage returns a Gemini transcript scoped to messages starting from
   266→// startMessageIndex. This is the Gemini equivalent of transcript.SliceFromLine —
   267→// for Gemini's single JSON blob, scoping is done by message index rather than line offset.
   268→// Returns the original data if startMessageIndex <= 0.
   269→// Returns nil if startMessageIndex exceeds the number of messages.
   270→func SliceFromMessage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) []byte {
   271→	if len(data) == 0 || startMessageIndex <= 0 {
   272→		return data
   273→	}
   274→
   275→	t, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	if startMessageIndex >= len(t.Messages) {
   281→		return nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	scoped := &GeminiTranscript{
   285→		Messages: t.Messages[startMessageIndex:],
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	out, err := json.Marshal(scoped)
   289→	if err != nil {
   290→		return nil
   291→	}
   292→	return out
   293→}
   294→
   295→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Gemini transcript.
   296→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message may have a tokens object
   297→// with input, output, cached, thoughts, tool, and total counts.
   298→// Only processes messages from startMessageIndex onwards (0-indexed).
   299→func CalculateTokenUsage(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   300→	var transcript struct {
   301→		Messages []geminiMessageWithTokens `json:"messages"`
   302→	}
   303→
   304→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &transcript); err != nil {
   305→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   306→	}
   307→
   308→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   309→
   310→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   311→		// Skip messages before startMessageIndex
   312→		if i < startMessageIndex {
   313→			continue
   314→		}
   315→
   316→		// Only count tokens from gemini (assistant) messages
   317→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   318→			continue
   319→		}
   320→
   321→		if msg.Tokens == nil {
   322→			continue
   323→		}
   324→
   325→		usage.APICallCount++
   326→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Tokens.Input
   327→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Tokens.Output
   328→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Tokens.Cached
   329→	}
   330→
   331→	return usage
   332→}
   333→
   334→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Gemini transcript file.
   335→// If startMessageIndex > 0, only considers messages from that index onwards.
   336→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startMessageIndex int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   337→	if path == "" {
   338→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   339→	}
   340→
   341→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   342→	if err != nil {
   343→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   344→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   345→		}
   346→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	return CalculateTokenUsage(data, startMessageIndex), nil
   350→}
   351→

    17→func TestInstallHooks_FreshInstall(t *testing.T) {
    18→	dir := t.TempDir()
    19→	t.Chdir(dir)
    20→	ag := &OpenCodeAgent{}
    21→
    22→	count, err := ag.InstallHooks(false, false)
    23→	if err != nil {

    30→	// Verify plugin file was created

    41→	}

     1→//go:build integration
     2→
     3→package integration
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"testing"
     7→
     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"

    12→
    13→// TestOpenCodeHookFlow verifies the full hook flow for OpenCode:
    14→// session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end → checkpoint → commit → condense → session-end.
    15→func TestOpenCodeHookFlow(t *testing.T) {
    16→	t.Parallel()
    17→
    18→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    19→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    20→
    21→		// Create OpenCode session
    22→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
    23→
    24→		// 1. session-start
    25→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    26→			t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
    27→		}
    28→
    29→		// 2. turn-start (equivalent to UserPromptSubmit — captures pre-prompt state)
    30→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
    31→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
    32→		}
    33→
    34→		// 3. Agent makes file changes (AFTER turn-start so they're detected as new)
    35→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
    36→
    37→		// 4. Create transcript with the file change
    38→		session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
    39→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
    40→		})
    41→
    42→		// 5. turn-end (equivalent to Stop — creates checkpoint)
    43→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    44→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
    45→		}
    46→
    47→		// 6. Verify checkpoint was created
    48→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
    49→		if len(points) == 0 {
    50→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
    51→		}
    52→
    53→		// 7. For manual-commit, user commits manually (triggers condensation).
    54→		// For auto-commit, the commit was already made during turn-end.
    55→		if strategyName == strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit {
    56→			env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add feature", "feature.go")
    57→		}
    58→
    59→		// 8. session-end
    60→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, session.TranscriptPath); err != nil {
    61→			t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
    62→		}
    63→
    64→		// 9. Verify condensation happened (checkpoint on metadata branch)
    65→		checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
    66→		if checkpointID == "" {
    67→			t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
    68→		}
    69→
    70→		// 10. Verify condensed data
    71→		transcriptPath := SessionFilePath(checkpointID, paths.TranscriptFileName)
    72→		_, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(paths.MetadataBranchName, transcriptPath)
    73→		if !found {
    74→			t.Error("condensed transcript should exist on metadata branch")
    75→		}
    76→	})
    77→}
    78→
    79→// TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition verifies that the OpenCode agent and strategy
    80→// work together correctly — agent parses session, strategy saves checkpoint, rewind works.
    81→func TestOpenCodeAgentStrategyComposition(t *testing.T) {
    82→	t.Parallel()
    83→
    84→	RunForAllStrategies(t, func(t *testing.T, env *TestEnv, strategyName string) {
    85→		env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategyName, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
    86→
    87→		ag, err := agent.Get("opencode")
    88→		if err != nil {
    89→			t.Fatalf("Get(opencode) error = %v", err)
    90→		}
    91→
    92→		_, err = strategy.Get(strategyName)
    93→		if err != nil {
    94→			t.Fatalf("Get(%s) error = %v", strategyName, err)
    95→		}
    96→
    97→		// Create session and transcript for agent interface testing.
    98→		// The transcript references feature.go but the actual file doesn't need
    99→		// to exist for ReadSession — it only parses the transcript JSONL.
   100→		session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   101→		transcriptPath := session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Add a feature", []FileChange{
   102→			{Path: "feature.go", Content: "package main\n// new feature"},
   103→		})
   104→
   105→		// Read session via agent interface
   106→		agentSession, err := ag.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   107→			SessionID:  session.ID,
   108→			SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   109→		})
   110→		if err != nil {
   111→			t.Fatalf("ReadSession() error = %v", err)
   112→		}
   113→
   114→		// Verify agent computed modified files
   115→		if len(agentSession.ModifiedFiles) == 0 {
   116→			t.Error("agent.ReadSession() should compute ModifiedFiles")
   117→		}
   118→
   119→		// Simulate session flow: session-start → turn-start → file changes → turn-end
   120→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   121→			t.Fatalf("session-start error = %v", err)
   122→		}
   123→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Add a feature"); err != nil {
   124→			t.Fatalf("turn-start error = %v", err)
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		// Create the actual file AFTER turn-start so the strategy detects it as new
   128→		env.WriteFile("feature.go", "package main\n// new feature")
   129→
   130→		if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   131→			t.Fatalf("turn-end error = %v", err)
   132→		}
   133→
   134→		// Verify checkpoint was created
   135→		points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   136→		if len(points) == 0 {
   137→			t.Fatal("expected at least 1 rewind point after turn-end")
   138→		}
   139→	})
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TestOpenCodeRewind verifies that rewind works with OpenCode checkpoints.
   143→func TestOpenCodeRewind(t *testing.T) {
   144→	t.Parallel()
   145→
   146→	// Test with manual-commit strategy as it has full file restoration on rewind
   147→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   148→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   149→
   150→	// First session
   151→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   152→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   153→
   154→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   155→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	// Turn 1: create file1.go (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   159→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create file1"); err != nil {
   160→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v1")
   164→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create file1", []FileChange{
   165→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v1"},
   166→	})
   167→
   168→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   169→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	points1 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   173→	if len(points1) == 0 {
   174→		t.Fatal("no rewind point after first turn")
   175→	}
   176→	checkpoint1ID := points1[0].ID
   177→
   178→	// Turn 2: modify file1 and create file2 (AFTER turn-start)
   179→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Modify file1"); err != nil {
   180→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	env.WriteFile("file1.go", "package main\n// file1 v2")
   184→	env.WriteFile("file2.go", "package main\n// file2")
   185→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Modify file1, create file2", []FileChange{
   186→		{Path: "file1.go", Content: "package main\n// file1 v2"},
   187→		{Path: "file2.go", Content: "package main\n// file2"},
   188→	})
   189→
   190→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   191→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	// Verify 2 checkpoints
   195→	points2 := env.GetRewindPoints()
   196→	if len(points2) < 2 {
   197→		t.Fatalf("expected at least 2 rewind points, got %d", len(points2))
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// Rewind to first checkpoint
   201→	if err := env.Rewind(checkpoint1ID); err != nil {
   202→		t.Fatalf("Rewind() error = %v", err)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	// Verify file1 is restored to v1
   206→	content := env.ReadFile("file1.go")
   207→	if content != "package main\n// file1 v1" {
   208→		t.Errorf("file1.go after rewind = %q, want v1 content", content)
   209→	}
   210→
   211→	// file2 should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1
   212→	if env.FileExists("file2.go") {
   213→		t.Error("file2.go should not exist after rewind to checkpoint 1")
   214→	}
   215→}
   216→
   217→// TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation verifies that multiple turns in a session
   218→// are correctly condensed when the user commits.
   219→func TestOpenCodeMultiTurnCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   220→	t.Parallel()
   221→
   222→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit)
   223→	env.InitEntireWithAgent(strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit, agent.AgentNameOpenCode)
   224→
   225→	session := env.NewOpenCodeSession()
   226→	transcriptPath := session.TranscriptPath
   227→
   228→	// session-start
   229→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionStart(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   230→		t.Fatalf("session-start error: %v", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Turn 1: create file (AFTER turn-start so it's detected as new)
   234→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnStart(session.ID, transcriptPath, "Create app.go"); err != nil {
   235→		t.Fatalf("turn-start error: %v", err)
   236→	}
   237→
   238→	env.WriteFile("app.go", "package main\nfunc main() {}")
   239→	session.CreateOpenCodeTranscript("Create app.go", []FileChange{
   240→		{Path: "app.go", Content: "package main\nfunc main() {}"},
   241→	})
   242→
   243→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   244→		t.Fatalf("turn-end error: %v", err)
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Verify checkpoint
   248→	points := env.GetRewindPoints()
   249→	if len(points) == 0 {
   250→		t.Fatal("expected rewind point after first turn")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Commit with hooks (triggers condensation)
   254→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Implement app", "app.go")
   255→
   256→	// session-end
   257→	if err := env.SimulateOpenCodeSessionEnd(session.ID, transcriptPath); err != nil {
   258→		t.Fatalf("session-end error: %v", err)
   259→	}
   260→
   261→	// Verify checkpoint was condensed to metadata branch
   262→	checkpointID := env.TryGetLatestCheckpointID()
   263→	if checkpointID == "" {
   264→		t.Fatal("expected checkpoint on metadata branch after commit")
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Verify files are on metadata branch
   268→	env.ValidateCheckpoint(CheckpointValidation{
   269→		CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   270→		Strategy:     strategy.StrategyNameManualCommit,
   271→		FilesTouched: []string{"app.go"},
   272→		ExpectedTranscriptContent: []string{
   273→			"Create app.go", // User prompt should appear in transcript
   274→		},
   275→	})
   276→}

  "old_string": "// runOpenCodeImport runs `opencode import <file>` to import a session into\n// OpenCode's database. The import preserves the original session ID\n// from the export file.\nfunc runOpenCodeImport(exportFilePath string) error {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"opencode\", \"import\", exportFilePath)\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"opencode import timed out after %s\", openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"opencode import failed: %w (output: %s)\", err, string(output))\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// runOpenCodeExport runs `opencode export <sessionID>` to export a session\n// from OpenCode's database. Returns the JSON export data as bytes.\nfunc runOpenCodeExport(sessionID string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"opencode\", \"export\", sessionID)\n\toutput, err := cmd.Output()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"opencode export timed out after %s\", openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Get stderr for better error message\n\t\tif exitErr, ok := err.(*exec.ExitError); ok {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"opencode export failed: %w (stderr: %s)\", err, string(exitErr.Stderr))\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"opencode export failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn output, nil\n}\n\n// runOpenCodeImport runs `opencode import <file>` to import a session into\n// OpenCode's database. The import preserves the original session ID\n// from the export file.\nfunc runOpenCodeImport(exportFilePath string) error {\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tcmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, \"opencode\", \"import\", exportFilePath)\n\tif output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {\n\t\tif ctx.Err() == context.DeadlineExceeded {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"opencode import timed out after %s\", openCodeCommandTimeout)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"opencode import failed: %w (output: %s)\", err, string(output))\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

{\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tPrompt         string `json:\"prompt\"`\n}\n\n// --- Transcript types (JSONL format — one Message per line) ---\n\n// Message role constants.\nconst (\n\troleAssistant = \"assistant\"\n\troleUser      = \"user\"\n)\n\n// Message represents a single message (one line) in the JSONL transcript.\ntype Message struct {\n\tID      string  `json:\"id\"`\n\tRole    string  `json:\"role\"` // \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n\tContent string  `json:\"content\"`\n\tTime    Time    `json:\"time\"`\n\tTokens  *Tokens `json:\"tokens,omitempty\"`\n\tCost    float64 `json:\"cost,omitempty\"`\n\tParts   []Part  `json:\"parts,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Time holds message timestamps.\ntype Time struct {\n\tCreated   int64 `json:\"created\"`\n\tCompleted int64 `json:\"completed,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Tokens holds token usage from assistant messages.\ntype Tokens struct {\n\tInput     int   `json:\"input\"`\n\tOutput    int   `json:\"output\"`\n\tReasoning int   `json:\"reasoning\"`\n\tCache     Cache `json:\"cache\"`\n}\n\n// Cache holds cache-related token counts.\ntype Cache struct {\n\tRead  int `json:\"read\"`\n\tWrite int `json:\"write\"`\n}\n\n// Part represents a message part (text, tool, etc.).\ntype Part struct {\n\tType   string     `json:\"type\"` // \"text\", \"tool\", etc.\n\tText   string     `json:\"text,omitempty\"`\n\tTool   string     `json:\"tool,omitempty\"`\n\tCallID string     `json:\"callID,omitempty\"`\n\tState  *ToolState `json:\"state,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// ToolState represents tool execution state.\ntype ToolState struct {\n\tStatus string                 `json:\"status\"` // \"pending\", \"running\", \"completed\", files on disk.\n// These match the actual tool names from OpenCode's source:\n//   - edit:  internal/llm/tools/edit.go  (EditToolName)\n//   - write: internal/llm/tools/write.go (WriteToolName)\n// `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n}\n\n// turnStartRaw matches the JSON payload for turn-start (user prompt submission).\ntype turnStartRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tPrompt         string `json:\"prompt\"`\n}\n\n// --- Export JSON types (from `opencode export`) ---\n\n// ExportSession represents the top-level structure of `opencode export` output.\n// This is OpenCode's native format for session data.\ntype ExportSession struct {\n\tInfo     SessionInfo     `json:\"info\"`\n\tMessages []ExportMessage `json:\"messages\"`\n}\n\n// SessionInfo contains session metadata from the export.\ntype SessionInfo struct {\n\tID        string `json:\"id\"`\n\tTitle     string `json:\"title,omitempty\"`\n\tCreatedAt int64  `json:\"createdAt,omitempty\"`\n\tUpdatedAt int64  `json:\"updatedAt,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// ExportMessage represents a single message in the export format.\n// Each message contains info (metadata) and parts (content).\ntype ExportMessage struct {\n\tInfo  MessageInfo `json:\"info\"`\n\tParts []Part      `json:\"parts\"`\n}\n\n// MessageInfo contains message metadata.\ntype MessageInfo struct {\n\tID        string  `json:\"id\"`\n\tSessionID string  `json:\"sessionID,omitempty\"`\n\tRole      string  `json:\"role\"` // \"user\" or \"assistant\"\n\tTime      Time    `json:\"time\"`\n\tTokens    *Tokens `json:\"tokens,omitempty\"`\n\tCost      float64 `json:\"cost,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Message role constants.\nconst (\n\troleAssistant = \"assistant\"\n\troleUser      = \"user\"\n)\n\n// Time holds message timestamps.\ntype Time struct {\n\tCreated   int64 `json:\"created\"`\n\tCompleted int64 `json:\"completed,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Tokens holds token usage from assistant messages.\ntype Tokens struct {\n\tInput     int   `json:\"input\"`\n\tOutput    int   `json:\"output\"`\n\tReasoning int   `json:\"reasoning\"`\n\tCache     Cache `json:\"cache\"`\n}\n\n// Cache holds cache-related token counts.\ntype Cache struct {\n\tRead  int `json:\"read\"`\n\tWrite int `json:\"write\"`\n}\n\n// Part represents a message part (text, tool, etc.).\ntype Part struct {\n\tType   string     `json:\"type\"` // \"text\", \"tool\", etc.\n\tText   string     `json:\"text,omitempty\"`\n\tTool   string     `json:\"tool,omitempty\"`\n\tCallID string     `json:\"callID,omitempty\"`\n\tState  *ToolState `json:\"state,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// ToolState represents tool execution state.\ntype ToolState struct {\n\tStatus string                 `json:\"status\"` // \"pending\", \"running\", \"completed\", \"error\"\n\tInput  map[string]interface{} `json:\"input,omitempty\"`\n\tOutput string                 `json:\"output,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// FileModificationTools are tools in OpenCode that modify files on disk.\n// These match the actual tool names from OpenCode's source:\n//   - edit:  internal/llm/tools/edit.go  (EditToolName)\n//   - write: internal/llm/tools/write.go (WriteToolName)\n//   - patch: internal/llm/tools/patch.go (PatchToolName)\nvar FileModificationTools = []string{\n\t\"edit\",\n\t\"write\",\n\t\"patch\",\n}"

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/transcript.go",
  "content": "package opencode\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"slices\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n)\n\n// Compile-time interface assertions\nvar (\n\t_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*OpenCodeAgent)(nil)\n)\n\n// ParseExportSession parses export JSON content into an ExportSession structure.\nfunc ParseExportSession(data []byte) (*ExportSession, error) {\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil for empty data is expected\n\t}\n\n\tvar session ExportSession\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &session); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse export session: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &session, nil\n}\n\n// parseExportSessionFromFile reads and parses an export JSON transcript file.\nfunc parseExportSessionFromFile(path string) (*ExportSession, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // Callers check os.IsNotExist on this error\n\t}\n\treturn ParseExportSession(data)\n}\n\n// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of messages in the transcript.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\tsession, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\treturn len(session.Messages), nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified by tool calls from the given message offset.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {\n\tsession, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, 0, err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tvar files []string\n\n\tfor i := startOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {\n\t\tmsg := session.Messages[i]\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, part := range msg.Parts {\n\t\t\tif part.Type != \"tool\" || part.State == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfilePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)\n\t\t\tif filePath != \"\" && !seen[filePath] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[filePath] = true\n\t\t\t\tfiles = append(files, filePath)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn files, len(session.Messages), nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts modified file paths from raw export JSON transcript bytes.\n// This is the bytes-based equivalent of ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, used by ReadSession.\nfunc ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {\n\tsession, err := ParseExportSession(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tvar files []string\n\n\tfor _, msg := range session.Messages {\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, part := range msg.Parts {\n\t\t\tif part.Type != \"tool\" || part.State == nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfilePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)\n\t\t\tif filePath != \"\" && !seen[filePath] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[filePath] = true\n\t\t\t\tfiles = append(files, filePath)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn files, nil\n}\n\n// extractFilePathFromInput extracts the file path from a tool's input map.\nfunc extractFilePathFromInput(input map[string]interface{}) string {\n\tfor _, key := range []string{\"file_path\", \"path\", \"file\", \"filename\"} {\n\t\tif v, ok := input[key]; ok {\n\t\t\tif s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn s\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompt strings from the transcript starting at the given offset.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tsession, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {\n\t\tmsg := session.Messages[i]\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Extract text from parts\n\t\tcontent := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)\n\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, content)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message content as a summary.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {\n\tsession, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := len(session.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tmsg := session.Messages[i]\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role == roleAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontent := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)\n\t\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn content, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n// extractTextFromParts extracts text content from message parts.\nfunc extractTextFromParts(parts []Part) string {\n\tvar texts []string\n\tfor _, part := range parts {\n\t\tif part.Type == \"text\" && part.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\ttexts = append(texts, part.Text)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Join(texts, \"\\n\")\n}\n\n// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from raw export JSON transcript bytes.\n// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path.\nfunc ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {\n\tsession, err := ParseExportSession(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor _, msg := range session.Messages {\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcontent := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)\n\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, content)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes computes token usage from raw export JSON transcript bytes\n// starting at the given message offset.\n// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path (which has bytes, not a file path).\nfunc CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {\n\tsession, err := ParseExportSession(data)\n\tif err != nil || session == nil {\n\t\treturn &agent.TokenUsage{}\n\t}\n\n\tusage := &agent.TokenUsage{}\n\tfor i := startMessageIndex; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {\n\t\tmsg := session.Messages[i]\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tusage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input\n\t\tusage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output\n\t\tusage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read\n\t\tusage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write\n\t\tusage.APICallCount++\n\t}\n\n\treturn usage\n}\n\n// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from assistant messages starting at the given offset.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {\n\tsession, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for nonexistent file is expected\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript for token usage: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for empty file is expected\n\t}\n\n\tusage := &agent.TokenUsage{}\n\tfor i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {\n\t\tmsg := session.Messages[i]\n\t\tif msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tusage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input\n\t\tusage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output\n\t\tusage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read\n\t\tusage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write\n\t\tusage.APICallCount++\n\t}\n\n\treturn usage, nil\n}\n"
}

    26→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &session); err != nil {
    27→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse export session: %w", err)
    28→	}
    29→
    30→	return &session, nil
    31→}
    32→
    33→// parseExportSessionFromFile reads and parses an export JSON transcript file.
    34→func parseExportSessionFromFile(path string) (*ExportSession, error) {
    35→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Path from agent hook
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // Callers check os.IsNotExist on this error
    38→	}
    39→	return ParseExportSession(data)
    40→}
    41→
    42→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the number of messages in the transcript.
    43→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
    44→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    47→			return 0, nil
    48→		}
    49→		return 0, err
    50→	}
    51→	if session == nil {
    52→		return 0, nil
    53→	}
    54→	return len(session.Messages), nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified by tool calls from the given message offset.
    58→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) ([]string, int, error) {
    59→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(path)
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    62→			return nil, 0, nil
    63→		}
    64→		return nil, 0, err
    65→	}
    66→	if session == nil {
    67→		return nil, 0, nil
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
    71→	var files []string
    72→
    73→	for i := startOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
    74→		msg := session.Messages[i]
    75→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
    76→			continue
    77→		}
    78→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
    79→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
    80→				continue
    81→			}
    82→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
    83→				continue
    84→			}
    85→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
    86→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
    87→				seen[filePath] = true
    88→				files = append(files, filePath)
    89→			}
    90→		}
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	return files, len(session.Messages), nil
    94→}
    95→
    96→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts modified file paths from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
    97→// This is the bytes-based equivalent of ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset, used by ReadSession.
    98→func ExtractModifiedFiles(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
    99→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return nil, err
   102→	}
   103→	if session == nil {
   104→		return nil, nil
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   108→	var files []string
   109→
   110→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   111→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant {
   112→			continue
   113→		}
   114→		for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   115→			if part.Type != "tool" || part.State == nil {
   116→				continue
   117→			}
   118→			if !slices.Contains(FileModificationTools, part.Tool) {
   119→				continue
   120→			}
   121→			filePath := extractFilePathFromInput(part.State.Input)
   122→			if filePath != "" && !seen[filePath] {
   123→				seen[filePath] = true
   124→				files = append(files, filePath)
   125→			}
   126→		}
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	return files, nil
   130→}
   131→
   132→// extractFilePathFromInput extracts the file path from a tool's input map.
   133→func extractFilePathFromInput(input map[string]interface{}) string {
   134→	for _, key := range []string{"file_path", "path", "file", "filename"} {
   135→		if v, ok := input[key]; ok {
   136→			if s, ok := v.(string); ok && s != "" {
   137→				return s
   138→			}
   139→		}
   140→	}
   141→	return ""
   142→}
   143→
   144→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompt strings from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   145→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
   146→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   147→	if err != nil {
   148→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   149→			return nil, nil
   150→		}
   151→		return nil, err
   152→	}
   153→	if session == nil {
   154→		return nil, nil
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	var prompts []string
   158→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   159→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   160→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   161→			continue
   162→		}
   163→		// Extract text from parts
   164→		content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   165→		if content != "" {
   166→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   167→		}
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	return prompts, nil
   171→}
   172→
   173→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message content as a summary.
   174→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
   175→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   176→	if err != nil {
   177→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   178→			return "", nil
   179→		}
   180→		return "", err
   181→	}
   182→	if session == nil {
   183→		return "", nil
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	for i := len(session.Messages) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   187→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   188→		if msg.Info.Role == roleAssistant {
   189→			content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   190→			if content != "" {
   191→				return content, nil
   192→			}
   193→		}
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	return "", nil
   197→}
   198→
   199→// extractTextFromParts extracts text content from message parts.
   200→func extractTextFromParts(parts []Part) string {
   201→	var texts []string
   202→	for _, part := range parts {
   203→		if part.Type == "text" && part.Text != "" {
   204→			texts = append(texts, part.Text)
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→	return strings.Join(texts, "\n")
   208→}
   209→
   210→// ExtractAllUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from raw export JSON transcript bytes.
   211→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path.
   212→func ExtractAllUserPrompts(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
   213→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   214→	if err != nil {
   215→		return nil, err
   216→	}
   217→	if session == nil {
   218→		return nil, nil
   219→	}
   220→
   221→	var prompts []string
   222→	for _, msg := range session.Messages {
   223→		if msg.Info.Role != roleUser {
   224→			continue
   225→		}
   226→		content := extractTextFromParts(msg.Parts)
   227→		if content != "" {
   228→			prompts = append(prompts, content)
   229→		}
   230→	}
   231→	return prompts, nil
   232→}
   233→
   234→// CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes computes token usage from raw export JSON transcript bytes
   235→// starting at the given message offset.
   236→// This is a package-level function used by the condensation path (which has bytes, not a file path).
   237→func CalculateTokenUsageFromBytes(data []byte, startMessageIndex int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   238→	session, err := ParseExportSession(data)
   239→	if err != nil || session == nil {
   240→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   244→	for i := startMessageIndex; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   245→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   246→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   247→			continue
   248→		}
   249→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   250→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   251→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   252→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   253→		usage.APICallCount++
   254→	}
   255→
   256→	return usage
   257→}
   258→
   259→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from assistant messages starting at the given offset.
   260→func (a *OpenCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   261→	session, err := parseExportSessionFromFile(sessionRef)
   262→	if err != nil {
   263→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   264→			return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for nonexistent file is expected
   265→		}
   266→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript for token usage: %w", err)
   267→	}
   268→	if session == nil {
   269→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil usage for empty file is expected
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   273→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(session.Messages); i++ {
   274→		msg := session.Messages[i]
   275→		if msg.Info.Role != roleAssistant || msg.Info.Tokens == nil {
   276→			continue
   277→		}
   278→		usage.InputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Input
   279→		usage.OutputTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Output
   280→		usage.CacheReadTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Read
   281→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += msg.Info.Tokens.Cache.Write
   282→		usage.APICallCount++
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	return usage, nil
   286→}
   287→

   171→
   172→// buildCondensedTranscriptFromOpenCode parses OpenCode JSONL transcript and extracts a condensed view.
   173→func buildCondensedTranscriptFromOpenCode(content []byte) ([]Entry, error) {
   174→	messages, err := opencode.ParseMessages(content)
   175→	if err != nil {
   176→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse OpenCode transcript: %w", err)
   177→	}
   178→
   179→	var entries []Entry
   180→	for _, msg := range messages {
   181→		switch msg.Role {
   182→		case "user":
   183→			if msg.Content != "" {
   184→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   185→					Type:    EntryTypeUser,
   186→					Content: msg.Content,
   187→				})
   188→			}
   189→		case "assistant":
   190→			if msg.Content != "" {
   191→				entries = append(entries, Entry{
   192→					Type:    EntryTypeAssistant,
   193→					Content: msg.Content,
   194→				})
   195→			}
   196→			for _, part := range msg.Parts {
   197→				if part.Type == "tool" && part.State != nil {
   198→					entries = append(entries, Entry{
   199→						Type:       EntryTypeTool,
   200→						ToolName:   part.Tool,
   201→						ToolDetail: extractGenericToolDetail(part.State.Input),
   202→					})
   203→				}
   204→			}
   205→		}
   206→	}
   207→
   208→	return entries, nil
   209→}
   210→
