     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.
     8→package cli
     9→
    10→import (
    11→	"context"
    12→	"errors"
    13→	"fmt"
    14→	"log/slog"
    15→	"os"
    16→	"path/filepath"
    17→	"strings"
    18→	"time"
    19→
    20→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    21→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    22→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    23→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    24→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    25→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    26→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    27→)
    28→
    29→// DispatchLifecycleEvent routes a normalized lifecycle event to the appropriate handler.
    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {
    33→		return errors.New("agent cannot be nil")
    34→	}
    35→	if event == nil {
    36→		return errors.New("event cannot be nil")
    37→	}
    38→
    39→	switch event.Type {
    40→	case agent.SessionStart:
    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)
    50→	case agent.SubagentStart:
    51→		return handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag, event)
    52→	case agent.SubagentEnd:
    53→		return handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag, event)
    54→	default:
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("unknown lifecycle event type: %d", event.Type)
    56→	}
    57→}
    58→
    59→// handleLifecycleSessionStart handles session start: shows banner, checks concurrent sessions,
    60→// fires state machine transition.
    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→// handleLifecycleTurnStart handles turn start: captures pre-prompt state,
   111→// ensures strategy setup, initializes session.
   112→func handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   113→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   114→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-start",
   115→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   116→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   117→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   118→	)
   119→
   120→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   121→	if sessionID == "" {
   122→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
   123→	}
   124→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Capture pre-prompt state (including transcript position via TranscriptAnalyzer)
   129→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(ag, sessionID, event.SessionRef); err != nil {
   130→		return err
   131→	}
   132→
   133→	// Ensure strategy setup and initialize session
   134→	strat := GetStrategy()
   135→
   136→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
   137→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
   138→	}
   139→
   140→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
   141→		agentType := ag.Type()
   142→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(sessionID, agentType, event.SessionRef, event.Prompt); err != nil {
   143→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
   144→		}
   145→	}
   146→
   147→	return nil
   148→}
   149→
   150→// handleLifecycleTurnEnd handles turn end: validates transcript, extracts metadata,
   151→// detects file changes, saves step + checkpoint, transitions phase.
   152→//
   153→//nolint:maintidx // high complexity due to sequential orchestration of 8 steps (validation, extraction, file detection, filtering, token calc, step save, phase transition, cleanup) - splitting would obscure the flow
   154→func handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   155→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   156→	logging.Info(logCtx, "turn-end",
   157→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   158→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   159→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
   160→	)
   161→
   162→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   163→	if sessionID == "" {
   164→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	transcriptRef := event.SessionRef
   168→	if transcriptRef == "" {
   169→		return errors.New("transcript file not specified")
   170→	}
   171→	if !fileExists(transcriptRef) {
   172→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found: %s", transcriptRef)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   176→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   177→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   178→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Create session metadata directory
   182→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   183→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir
   186→	}
   187→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// If agent implements TranscriptPreparer, wait for transcript to be ready
   192→	if preparer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptPreparer); ok {
   193→		if err := preparer.PrepareTranscript(transcriptRef); err != nil {
   194→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to prepare transcript: %v\n", err)
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Copy transcript to session directory
   199→	transcriptData, err := ag.ReadTranscript(transcriptRef)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   204→	if err := os.WriteFile(logFile, transcriptData, 0o600); err != nil {
   205→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   206→	}
   207→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   208→
   209→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on TurnStart)
   210→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Determine transcript offset
   216→	transcriptOffset := resolveTranscriptOffset(preState, sessionID)
   217→
   218→	// Extract metadata via agent interface (prompts, summary, modified files)
   219→	var allPrompts []string
   220→	var summary string
   221→	var modifiedFiles []string
   222→	var newTranscriptPosition int
   223→
   224→	// Compute subagents directory for agents that support subagent extraction.
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptRef), event.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→
   228→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   229→		// Extract prompts
   230→		if prompts, promptErr := analyzer.ExtractPrompts(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); promptErr != nil {
   231→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract prompts: %v\n", promptErr)
   232→		} else {
   233→			allPrompts = prompts
   234→		}
   235→
   236→		// Extract summary
   237→		if s, sumErr := analyzer.ExtractSummary(transcriptRef); sumErr != nil {
   238→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract summary: %v\n", sumErr)
   239→		} else {
   240→			summary = s
   241→		}
   242→
   243→		// Extract modified files - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor if available to include subagent files
   244→		if subagentExtractor, subOk := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); subOk {
   245→			if files, fileErr := subagentExtractor.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir); fileErr != nil {
   246→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files (with subagents): %v\n", fileErr)
   247→			} else {
   248→				modifiedFiles = files
   249→			}
   250→			// Get position from basic analyzer
   251→			if _, pos, posErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); posErr == nil {
   252→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   253→			}
   254→		} else {
   255→			// Fall back to basic extraction (main transcript only)
   256→			if files, pos, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptRef, transcriptOffset); fileErr != nil {
   257→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files: %v\n", fileErr)
   258→			} else {
   259→				modifiedFiles = files
   260→				newTranscriptPosition = pos
   261→			}
   262→		}
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	// Write prompts file
   266→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   267→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   268→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   269→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   272→
   273→	// Write summary file
   274→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   275→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   276→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   277→	}
   278→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   279→
   280→	// Generate commit message from last prompt
   281→	lastPrompt := ""
   282→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   283→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   284→	}
   285→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   286→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   287→
   288→	// Get repo root for path normalization
   289→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   295→	if preState != nil {
   296→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   297→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   298→	}
   299→
   300→	// Detect file changes via git status
   301→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   302→	if err != nil {
   303→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Filter and normalize all paths
   307→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   308→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   309→	if changes != nil {
   310→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   311→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	// Filter transcript-extracted files to exclude files already committed to HEAD.
   315→	// When an agent commits files mid-turn, those files are condensed by PostCommit
   316→	// and should not be re-added to FilesTouched by SaveStep. A file is "committed"
   317→	// if it exists in HEAD with the same content as the working tree.
   318→	relModifiedFiles = filterToUncommittedFiles(relModifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   319→
   320→	// Check if there are any changes
   321→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   322→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   323→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   324→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   325→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   326→		if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   327→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   328→		}
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Log file changes
   333→	logFileChanges(relModifiedFiles, relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles)
   334→
   335→	// Create context file
   336→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   337→	if err := createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, allPrompts, summary); err != nil {
   338→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   339→	}
   340→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   341→
   342→	// Get git author
   343→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   344→	if err != nil {
   345→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   346→	}
   347→
   348→	// Get strategy and agent type
   349→	strat := GetStrategy()
   350→	agentType := ag.Type()
   351→
   352→	// Get transcript position/identifier from pre-prompt state
   353→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   354→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   355→	if preState != nil {
   356→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   357→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.TranscriptOffset
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Calculate token usage - prefer SubagentAwareExtractor to include subagent tokens
   361→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   362→	if subagentExtractor, ok := ag.(agent.SubagentAwareExtractor); ok {
   363→		usage, tokenErr := subagentExtractor.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   364→		if tokenErr != nil {
   365→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage (with subagents): %v\n", tokenErr)
   366→		} else {
   367→			tokenUsage = usage
   368→		}
   369→	} else if calculator, ok := ag.(agent.TokenCalculator); ok {
   370→		// Fall back to basic token calculation (main transcript only)
   371→		usage, tokenErr := calculator.CalculateTokenUsage(transcriptRef, transcriptLinesAtStart)
   372→		if tokenErr != nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   374→		} else {
   375→			tokenUsage = usage
   376→		}
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Build fully-populated step context and delegate to strategy
   380→	ctx := strategy.StepContext{
   381→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   382→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   383→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   384→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   385→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   386→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   387→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   388→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptRef,
   389→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   390→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   391→		AgentType:                agentType,
   392→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   393→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   394→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   395→	}
   396→
   397→	if err := strat.SaveStep(ctx); err != nil {
   398→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save step: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Update session state transcript position for auto-commit strategy
   402→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit && newTranscriptPosition > 0 {
   403→		updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID, newTranscriptPosition)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	// Transition session phase and cleanup pre-prompt state
   407→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   408→	if cleanupErr := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); cleanupErr != nil {
   409→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", cleanupErr)
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	return nil
   413→}
   414→
   415→// handleLifecycleCompaction handles context compaction: saves current progress
   416→// but stays in ACTIVE phase (unlike TurnEnd which transitions to IDLE).
   417→// Also resets the transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated.
   418→func handleLifecycleCompaction(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   419→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   420→	logging.Info(logCtx, "compaction",
   421→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   422→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   423→	)
   424→
   425→	// Fire EventCompaction to trigger ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched (stays in ACTIVE)
   426→	sessionID := event.SessionID
   427→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   428→	if loadErr != nil {
   429→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for compaction: %v\n", loadErr)
   430→	}
   431→	if sessionState != nil {
   432→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(sessionState, session.EventCompaction, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   433→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: compaction transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   434→		}
   435→
   436→		// Reset transcript offset since the transcript may be truncated/reorganized
   437→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 0
   438→
   439→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); saveErr != nil {
   440→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save session state after compaction: %v\n", saveErr)
   441→		}
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Context compaction: transcript offset reset\n")
   445→	return nil
   446→}
   447→
   448→// handleLifecycleSessionEnd handles session end: marks the session as ended.
   449→func handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   450→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   451→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   452→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   453→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   454→	)
   455→
   456→	if event.SessionID == "" {
   457→		return nil // No session to update
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Note: We intentionally don't clean up cached transcripts here.
   461→	// Post-session commits (carry-forward in ENDED phase) may still need
   462→	// the transcript to extract file changes. Cleanup is handled by
   463→	// `entire clean` or when the session state is fully removed.
   464→
   465→	if err := markSessionEnded(event.SessionID); err != nil {
   466→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   467→	}
   468→	return nil
   469→}
   470→
   471→// handleLifecycleSubagentStart handles subagent start: captures pre-task state.
   472→func handleLifecycleSubagentStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   473→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   474→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-start",
   475→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   476→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   477→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   478→	)
   479→
   480→	// Log context
   481→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent started\n")
   482→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   483→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   484→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Transcript: %s\n", event.SessionRef)
   485→
   486→	// Capture pre-task state
   487→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(event.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   488→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   489→	}
   490→
   491→	return nil
   492→}
   493→
   494→// handleLifecycleSubagentEnd handles subagent end: detects changes, saves task checkpoint.
   495→func handleLifecycleSubagentEnd(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
   496→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
   497→	logging.Info(logCtx, "subagent-end",
   498→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
   499→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
   500→		slog.String("tool_use_id", event.ToolUseID),
   501→		slog.String("subagent_id", event.SubagentID),
   502→	)
   503→
   504→	// Extract subagent type and description from tool input
   505→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(event.ToolInput)
   506→
   507→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   508→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(event.SessionRef)
   509→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   510→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   511→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, event.SubagentID)
   512→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   513→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   514→		}
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	// Log context
   518→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Subagent completed\n")
   519→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", event.SessionID)
   520→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Tool Use ID: %s\n", event.ToolUseID)
   521→	if event.SubagentID != "" {
   522→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Agent ID: %s\n", event.SubagentID)
   523→	}
   524→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   525→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Subagent Transcript: %s\n", subagentTranscriptPath)
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	// Extract modified files from subagent transcript
   529→	var modifiedFiles []string
   530→	if analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer); ok {
   531→		transcriptToScan := event.SessionRef
   532→		if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   533→			transcriptToScan = subagentTranscriptPath
   534→		}
   535→		if files, _, fileErr := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(transcriptToScan, 0); fileErr != nil {
   536→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files from subagent: %v\n", fileErr)
   537→		} else {
   538→			modifiedFiles = files
   539→		}
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Load pre-task state and detect file changes
   543→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID)
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   546→	}
   547→	var preUntrackedFiles []string
   548→	if preState != nil {
   549→		preUntrackedFiles = preState.PreUntrackedFiles()
   550→	}
   551→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preUntrackedFiles)
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Get repo root and normalize paths
   557→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   558→	if err != nil {
   559→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   563→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   564→	if changes != nil {
   565→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   566→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   567→	}
   568→
   569→	// If no changes, skip
   570→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   571→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   572→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   573→		return nil
   574→	}
   575→
   576→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort)
   577→	var checkpointUUID string
   578→	// Use the existing CLI-level checkpoint UUID finder
   579→	mainLines, _, _ := parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(event.SessionRef) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort
   580→	if mainLines != nil {
   581→		checkpointUUID, _ = FindCheckpointUUID(mainLines, event.ToolUseID)
   582→	}
   583→
   584→	// Get git author
   585→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   586→	if err != nil {
   587→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   588→	}
   589→
   590→	// Build task checkpoint context
   591→	strat := GetStrategy()
   592→	agentType := ag.Type()
   593→
   594→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   595→		SessionID:              event.SessionID,
   596→		ToolUseID:              event.ToolUseID,
   597→		AgentID:                event.SubagentID,
   598→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   599→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   600→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   601→		TranscriptPath:         event.SessionRef,
   602→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   603→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   604→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   605→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   606→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   607→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   608→		AgentType:              agentType,
   609→	}
   610→
   611→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   612→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task step: %w", err)
   613→	}
   614→
   615→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(event.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   616→	return nil
   617→}
   618→
   619→// --- Helper functions ---
   620→
   621→// resolveTranscriptOffset determines the transcript offset to use for parsing.
   622→// Prefers pre-prompt state, falls back to session state.
   623→func resolveTranscriptOffset(preState *PrePromptState, sessionID string) int {
   624→	if preState != nil && preState.TranscriptOffset > 0 {
   625→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", preState.TranscriptOffset)
   626→		return preState.TranscriptOffset
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   630→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   631→	if loadErr != nil {
   632→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   633→		return 0
   634→	}
   635→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   636→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from offset %d\n", sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   637→		return sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   638→	}
   639→
   640→	return 0
   641→}
   642→
   643→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   644→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   645→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   646→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   647→	if loadErr != nil {
   648→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   649→		return
   650→	}
   651→	if sessionState == nil {
   652→		sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   653→			SessionID: sessionID,
   654→		}
   655→	}
   656→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   657→	sessionState.StepCount++
   658→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   659→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   660→	} else {
   661→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   662→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   663→	}
   664→}
   665→
   666→// createContextFile creates a context.md file for the session checkpoint.
   667→// This is a unified version that works for all agents.
   668→func createContextFile(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID string, prompts []string, summary string) error {
   669→	var sb strings.Builder
   670→
   671→	sb.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   672→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   673→	fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "Commit Message: %s\n\n", commitMessage)
   674→
   675→	if len(prompts) > 0 {
   676→		sb.WriteString("## Prompts\n\n")
   677→		for i, p := range prompts {
   678→			fmt.Fprintf(&sb, "### Prompt %d\n\n%s\n\n", i+1, p)
   679→		}
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	if summary != "" {
   683→		sb.WriteString("## Summary\n\n")
   684→		sb.WriteString(summary)
   685→		sb.WriteString("\n")
   686→	}
   687→
   688→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(sb.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   689→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   690→	}
   691→	return nil
   692→}
   693→
   694→// parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID is a thin wrapper around transcript parsing for checkpoint UUID lookup.
   695→// Returns parsed transcript lines for use with FindCheckpointUUID.
   696→func parseTranscriptForCheckpointUUID(transcriptPath string) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
   697→	lines, total, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   698→	if err != nil {
   699→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("parsing transcript for checkpoint UUID: %w", err)
   700→	}
   701→	return lines, total, nil
   702→}
   703→
   704→// transitionSessionTurnEnd transitions the session phase to IDLE and dispatches turn-end actions.
   705→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   706→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   707→	if loadErr != nil {
   708→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   709→		return
   710→	}
   711→	if turnState == nil {
   712→		return
   713→	}
   714→	if err := strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); err != nil {
   715→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end transition failed: %v\n", err)
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   719→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   720→	strat := GetStrategy()
   721→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   722→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   723→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   724→		}
   725→	}
   726→
   727→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   728→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   729→	}
   730→}
   731→
   732→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   733→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   734→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   735→	if err != nil {
   736→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   737→	}
   738→	if state == nil {
   739→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   740→	}
   741→
   742→	if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   743→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session stop transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	now := time.Now()
   747→	state.EndedAt = &now
   748→
   749→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   750→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   751→	}
   752→	return nil
   753→}
   754→
   755→// logFileChanges logs the files modified, created, and deleted during a session.
   756→func logFileChanges(modified, newFiles, deleted []string) {
   757→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(modified))
   758→	for _, file := range modified {
   759→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   760→	}
   761→	if len(newFiles) > 0 {
   762→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(newFiles))
   763→		for _, file := range newFiles {
   764→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   765→		}
   766→	}
   767→	if len(deleted) > 0 {
   768→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(deleted))
   769→		for _, file := range deleted {
   770→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   771→		}
   772→	}
   773→}
   774→

**Issue 1**: Stale cached transcript - `fetchAndCacheExport` returns existing files without refreshing, causing stale data on subsequent turns.

**Issue 2**: `countTranscriptItems` doesn't handle OpenCode's JSON format - it falls through to JSONL line counting.

**Issue 3**: The comment in `WriteSession` says "JSONL" but the format is actually JSON.

   499→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   500→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   501→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   502→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   503→	if content == "" {
   504→		return 0
   505→	}
   506→
   507→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   508→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   509→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   510→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   511→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   512→		}
   513→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   514→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   515→			return 0
   516→		}
   517→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   518→	}
   519→
   520→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   521→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   522→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   523→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   524→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   525→	}
   526→	return len(allLines)
   527→}

This allows tests to provide mock transcript data.\n// See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd for the test helper\n// that uses this seam.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {\n\t// Get repo root for the temp directory\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\ttmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)\n\ttmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+\".json\")\n\n\t// Check if file already exists (e.g., pre-written by integration tests)\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {\n\t\treturn tmpFile, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Call opencode export to get the transcript\n\tdata, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"opencode export failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write to temp directory under .entire\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create temp dir: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write export file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tmpFile, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// fetchAndCacheExport calls `opencode export <sessionID>` and writes the result\n// to a temporary file. Returns the path to the temp file.\n//\n// Integration testing: Set ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1 to skip the\n// `opencode export` call and use pre-written mock data instead. Tests must\n// pre-write the transcript file to .entire/tmp/<sessionID>.json before\n// triggering the hook. See integration_test/hooks.go:SimulateOpenCodeTurnEnd.\nfunc (a *OpenCodeAgent) fetchAndCacheExport(sessionID string) (string, error) {\n\t// Get repo root for the temp directory\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\ttmpDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, paths.EntireTmpDir)\n\ttmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, sessionID+\".json\")\n\n\t// Integration test mode: use pre-written mock file without calling opencode export\n\tif os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT\") != \"\" {\n\t\tif _, err := os.Stat(tmpFile); err == nil {\n\t\t\treturn tmpFile, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"mock export file not found: %s (ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT is set)\", tmpFile)\n\t}\n\n\t// Call opencode export to get the transcript (always refresh on each turn)\n\tdata, err := runOpenCodeExport(sessionID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"opencode export failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Write to temp directory under .entire\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(tmpDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create temp dir: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write export file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tmpFile, nil\n}"
}

  "old_string": "// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.\n// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.\n// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.\n// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.\nfunc countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {\n\tif content == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\n\t// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown\n\tif agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {\n\t\ttranscript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))\n\t\tif err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn len(transcript.Messages)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0\n\t\tif agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {\n\t\t\treturn 0\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type\n\t}\n\n\t// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents\n\tallLines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\t// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \\n in JSONL)\n\tfor len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == \"\" {\n\t\tallLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]\n\t}\n\treturn len(allLines)\n}",
  "new_string": "// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.\n// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.\n// For Gemini CLI, OpenCode, and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.\n// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.\nfunc countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {\n\tif content == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\n\t// OpenCode uses export JSON format with {\"info\": {...}, \"messages\": [...]}\n\tif agentType == agent.AgentTypeOpenCode {\n\t\tsession, err := opencode.ParseExportSession([]byte(content))\n\t\tif err == nil && session != nil {\n\t\t\treturn len(session.Messages)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0\n\t}\n\n\t// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown\n\tif agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {\n\t\ttranscript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))\n\t\tif err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {\n\t\t\treturn len(transcript.Messages)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0\n\t\tif agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {\n\t\t\treturn 0\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type\n\t}\n\n\t// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents\n\tallLines := strings.Split(content, \"\\n\")\n\t// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \\n in JSONL)\n\tfor len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == \"\" {\n\t\tallLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]\n\t}\n\treturn len(allLines)\n}"
}

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the three high-severity bugs I fixed:

## Fixes Applied

### 1. Stale cached transcript (High Severity)
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/lifecycle.go`

The `fetchAndCacheExport` function was returning pre-existing files without refreshing, causing stale transcript data on subsequent turns. 

**Fix:** Changed the logic to always call `opencode export` during actual hook calls. Integration tests now use `ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT=1` to enable pre-written mock files.

### 2. OpenCode message count using JSONL line counting (High Severity)  
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go`

The `countTranscriptItems` function was falling through to JSONL line counting for OpenCode, but OpenCode uses JSON format with a messages array.

**Fix:** Added explicit handling for `agent.AgentTypeOpenCode` that parses the export JSON and counts messages correctly.

### 3. Misleading JSONL comment (Low Severity)
**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/opencode/opencode.go`

The comment said "Write JSONL file" but the format is actually JSON.

**Fix:** Updated comment to say "Write export JSON file".

### Additional Changes
- Updated `cmd/entire/cli/integration_test/hooks.go` to set the `ENTIRE_TEST_OPENCODE_MOCK_EXPORT` environment variable for integration tests
- Added test cases for OpenCode in `TestCountTranscriptItems` to ensure proper coverage
